\displaystyle+\|\overline{\pi}_{v}\|+\sum_{\mathcal{A},\mathcal{B}\in\overline{\pi}_{v}}\xi_{\overline{\pi}_{v}}(\mathcal{A})\xi_{\overline{\pi}_{v}}(\mathcal{B})h^{0}\left(\sum_{i=1}^{3}\mathcal{O}_{p_{v}}(\mathcal{A}-\mathcal{B}+\mathcal{D}_{i,v})\right)

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=1}^{3}\sum_{\mathcal{A}\in\lambda_{i,v}}\sum_{\mathcal{B}\in\overline{\pi}_{v}}\xi_{\overline{\pi}_{v}}(\mathcal{B})h^{0}\left(\mathcal{O}_{p_{v}}(\mathcal{A}-\mathcal{B})\otimes\lambda_{-1}N_{\mathcal{C}_{e_{i,v}}/\mathcal{X}}\right)

\displaystyle+\sum_{i\neq j}\sum_{\mathcal{A}\in\lambda_{i,v}}\sum_{\mathcal{B}\in\lambda_{j,v}}h^{0}(\mathcal{O}_{p_{v}}(\mathcal{A}-\mathcal{B}+\mathcal{D}_{j,v}))

and

\displaystyle\mathbf{E}(e)=\sum_{\mathcal{A},\mathcal{B}\in\lambda_{e}}\chi(\mathcal{O}_{\mathcal{C}_{e}}(\mathcal{A}-\mathcal{B})+\mathcal{O}_{\mathcal{C}_{e}}(\mathcal{A}-\mathcal{B}+\mathcal{D}_{e})).

Proof.

By [
18
, Lemma 5.16], we have the following equality

\displaystyle\mathrm{Ext}^{1}(\mathbf{I}^{\bullet},\mathbf{I}^{\bullet})_{0}-\mathrm{Ext}^{2}(\mathbf{I}^{\bullet},\mathbf{I}^{\bullet})_{0}=\widetilde{\chi}(\mathcal{O}_{\mathcal{X}},\mathcal{O}_{\mathcal{X}})-\widetilde{\chi}(\mathbf{I}^{\bullet},\mathbf{I}^{\bullet}).

As there exist an exact triangle in
\mathrm{D}^{b}(\mathcal{X})
:

\displaystyle\mathcal{F}[-1]\to\mathbf{I}^{\bullet}\to\mathcal{O}_{\mathcal{X}}\to\mathcal{F},

and a short exact sequence

\displaystyle 0\to\mathcal{O}_{\mathcal{C}_{\mathcal{F}}}\to\mathcal{F}\to\mathtt{Q}\to 0,

then by [
7
, Proposition 6-(1)], we have

\displaystyle\mathrm{Ext}^{1}(\mathbf{I}^{\bullet},\mathbf{I}^{\bullet})_{0}-\left(\mathrm{Ext}^{1}(\mathbf{I}^{\bullet},\mathbf{I}^{\bullet})_{0}\right)^{*}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\widetilde{\chi}(\mathcal{O}_{\mathcal{X}},\mathcal{O}_{\mathcal{X}})-\widetilde{\chi}(\mathbf{I}^{\bullet},\mathbf{I}^{\bullet})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\widetilde{\chi}(\mathcal{O}_{\mathcal{X}},\mathcal{O}_{\mathcal{C}_{\mathcal{F}}})-\left(\widetilde{\chi}(\mathcal{O}_{\mathcal{X}},\mathcal{O}_{\mathcal{C}_{\mathcal{F}}})\right)^{*}+\widetilde{\chi}(\mathcal{O}_{\mathcal{X}},\mathtt{Q})-\left(\widetilde{\chi}(\mathcal{O}_{\mathcal{X}},\mathtt{Q})\right)^{*}

\displaystyle-\left(\widetilde{\chi}(\mathcal{O}_{\mathcal{C}_{\mathcal{F}}},\mathtt{Q})-\left(\widetilde{\chi}(\mathcal{O}_{\mathcal{C}_{\mathcal{F}}},\mathtt{Q})\right)^{*}\right)-\widetilde{\chi}(\mathcal{O}_{\mathcal{C}_{\mathcal{F}}},\mathcal{O}_{\mathcal{C}_{\mathcal{F}}})-\widetilde{\chi}(\mathtt{Q},\mathtt{Q})

and

\displaystyle s_{1}(\mathrm{Ext}^{1}(\mathbf{I}^{\bullet},\mathbf{I}^{\bullet})_{0})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle s_{1}(\widetilde{\chi}(\mathcal{O}_{\mathcal{X}},\mathcal{O}_{\mathcal{C}_{\mathcal{F}}}))+s_{1}(\widetilde{\chi}(\mathcal{O}_{\mathcal{X}},\mathtt{Q}))+s_{1}(\widetilde{\chi}(\mathcal{O}_{\mathcal{C}_{\mathcal{F}}},\mathtt{Q}))+\sigma(\widetilde{\chi}(\mathcal{O}_{\mathcal{C}_{\mathcal{F}}},\mathcal{O}_{\mathcal{C}_{\mathcal{F}}}))+\sigma(\widetilde{\chi}(\mathtt{Q},\mathtt{Q}))

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle s_{2}(\chi(\mathcal{O}_{\mathcal{C}_{\mathcal{F}}}))+s_{2}(\chi(\mathtt{Q}))+s_{1}(\widetilde{\chi}(\mathcal{O}_{\mathcal{C}_{\mathcal{F}}},\mathtt{Q}))+\sigma(\widetilde{\chi}(\mathcal{O}_{\mathcal{C}_{\mathcal{F}}},\mathcal{O}_{\mathcal{C}_{\mathcal{F}}}))+\sigma(\widetilde{\chi}(\mathtt{Q},\mathtt{Q})).

As in the proof of Proposition
3.15
, we have

\displaystyle\mathcal{O}_{\mathcal{C}_{\mathcal{F}}}=\sum_{v\in V_{\mathcal{X}}}\sum_{\mathcal{D}\in\overline{\pi}_{v}}\xi_{\overline{\pi}_{v}}(\mathcal{D})\mathcal{O}_{p_{v}}(-\mathcal{D})+\sum_{e\in E_{\mathcal{X}}}\sum_{\mathcal{D}\in\lambda_{e}}\mathcal{O}_{\mathcal{C}_{e}}(-\mathcal{D}).

It is proved in [
7
, Section 6.2] that

\displaystyle\sigma(\widetilde{\chi}(\mathcal{O}_{\mathcal{C}_{\mathcal{F}}},\mathcal{O}_{\mathcal{C}_{\mathcal{F}}}))

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{e\in E_{\mathcal{X}}}s_{2}\bigg(\sum_{\mathcal{A},\mathcal{B}\in\lambda_{e}}\chi(\mathcal{O}_{\mathcal{C}_{e}}(\mathcal{A}-\mathcal{B})+\mathcal{O}_{\mathcal{C}_{e}}(\mathcal{A}-\mathcal{B}+\mathcal{D}_{e}))\bigg)

\displaystyle+\sum_{v\in V_{\mathcal{X}}}s_{2}\Bigg(\|\overline{\pi}_{v}\|+\sum_{\mathcal{A},\mathcal{B}\in\overline{\pi}_{v}}\xi_{\overline{\pi}_{v}}(\mathcal{A})\xi_{\overline{\pi}_{v}}(\mathcal{B})h^{0}\left(\sum_{i=1}^{3}\mathcal{O}_{p_{v}}(\mathcal{A}-\mathcal{B}+\mathcal{D}_{i,v})\right)

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=1}^{3}\sum_{\mathcal{A}\in\lambda_{i,v}}\sum_{\mathcal{B}\in\overline{\pi}_{v}}\xi_{\overline{\pi}_{v}}(\mathcal{B})h^{0}\left(\mathcal{O}_{p_{v}}(\mathcal{A}-\mathcal{B})\otimes\lambda_{-1}N_{\mathcal{C}_{e_{i,v}}/\mathcal{X}}\right)

\displaystyle+\sum_{i\neq j}\sum_{\mathcal{A}\in\lambda_{i,v}}\sum_{\mathcal{B}\in\lambda_{j,v}}h^{0}(\mathcal{O}_{p_{v}}(\mathcal{A}-\mathcal{B}+\mathcal{D}_{j,v}))\Bigg).

By Proposition
3.15
and using the similar argument as in [
7
, Section 6.2], we have

\displaystyle\chi(\mathtt{Q})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{v\in V_{\mathcal{X}}}\sum_{\mathcal{A}\in\widetilde{\pi}_{v}}\eta_{\widetilde{\pi}_{v}}(\mathcal{A})h^{0}\left(\mathcal{O}_{p_{v}}(-\mathcal{A})\right),

\displaystyle\sigma\left(\widetilde{\chi}(\mathtt{Q},\mathtt{Q})\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{v\in V_{\mathcal{X}}}s_{2}\left(\ell(\widetilde{\pi}_{v})+\sum_{\mathcal{A},\mathcal{B}\in\widetilde{\pi}_{v}}\eta_{\widetilde{\pi}_{v}}(\mathcal{A})\eta_{\widetilde{\pi}_{v}}(\mathcal{B})h^{0}\left(\sum_{i=1}^{3}\mathcal{O}_{p_{v}}(\mathcal{A}-\mathcal{B}+\mathcal{D}_{i,v})\right)\right)

and

\displaystyle s_{1}(\widetilde{\chi}(\mathcal{O}_{\mathcal{C}_{\mathcal{F}}},\mathtt{Q}))

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{v\in V_{\mathcal{X}}}s_{2}\left(\sum_{\mathcal{A}\in\widetilde{\pi}_{v}}\sum_{\mathcal{B}\in\overline{\pi}_{v}}\eta_{\widetilde{\pi}_{v}}(\mathcal{A})\xi_{\overline{\pi}_{v}}(\mathcal{B})h^{0}\left(\sum_{i=1}^{3}\mathcal{O}_{p_{v}}(\mathcal{A}-\mathcal{B}+\mathcal{D}_{i,v})\right)\right)

\displaystyle+\sum_{v\in V_{\mathcal{X}}}s_{2}\left(\sum_{\mathcal{A}\in\overline{\pi}_{v}}\sum_{\mathcal{B}\in\widetilde{\pi}_{v}}\xi_{\overline{\pi}_{v}}(\mathcal{A})\eta_{\widetilde{\pi}_{v}}(\mathcal{B})h^{0}\left(\sum_{i=1}^{3}\mathcal{O}_{p_{v}}(\mathcal{A}-\mathcal{B}+\mathcal{D}_{i,v})\right)\right)

\displaystyle+\sum_{v\in V_{\mathcal{X}}}s_{2}\left(\sum_{i=1}^{3}\sum_{\mathcal{A}\in\lambda_{i,v}}\sum_{\mathcal{B}\in\widetilde{\pi}_{v}}\eta_{\widetilde{\pi}_{v}}(\mathcal{B})h^{0}\left(\mathcal{O}_{p_{v}}(\mathcal{A}-\mathcal{B})\otimes\lambda_{-1}(N_{\mathcal{C}_{e_{i,v}}/\mathcal{X}})\right)\right).

Now the proof is completed by combining all the above results. ∎

Remark 3.19
.

The pairity of
\dim\mathrm{Ext}^{1}(\mathbf{I}^{\bullet},\mathbf{I}^{\bullet})_{0}
in Theorem
3.18
have the contribution from the edge part
\mathbf{E}(e)
which is the same as
\mathbf{SE}_{\lambda(e)}(e)
in DT case [
7
, Theorem 21], while its vertex part
\mathbf{V}(v)
is more complicated involving labelled box configurations
\widetilde{\pi}_{v}
due to the first 6 terms. The sum of the last 4 terms in
\mathbf{V}(v)
is exactly the vertex part of DT side
\mathbf{SV}_{\pi(v)}(v)
with
\pi(v)=\overline{\pi}_{v}
. The formula for
\mathbf{V}(v)
is arranged for comparing with the DT case and noticing the computation needed only for the additional terms later.
Actually, as in Remark
3.16
, the formula
\mathbf{V}(v)
can be rewritten as

\displaystyle\mathbf{V}(v)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\ell(\widetilde{\pi}_{v})+\|\overline{\pi}_{v}\|+\sum_{\mathcal{A}\in\widetilde{\pi}_{v}}\eta_{\widetilde{\pi}_{v}}(\mathcal{A})h^{0}\left(\mathcal{O}_{p_{v}}(-\mathcal{A})\right)

\displaystyle+\sum_{\mathcal{A},\mathcal{B}\in\breve{\pi}_{v}}\zeta_{\breve{\pi}_{v}}(\mathcal{A})\zeta_{\breve{\pi}_{v}}(\mathcal{B})h^{0}\left(\sum_{i=1}^{3}\mathcal{O}_{p_{v}}(\mathcal{A}-\mathcal{B}+\mathcal{D}_{i,v})\right)

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=1}^{3}\sum_{\mathcal{A}\in\lambda_{i,v}}\sum_{\mathcal{B}\in\breve{\pi}_{v}}\zeta_{\breve{\pi}_{v}}(\mathcal{B})h^{0}\left(\mathcal{O}_{p_{v}}(\mathcal{A}-\mathcal{B})\otimes\lambda_{-1}(N_{\mathcal{C}_{e_{i,v}}/\mathcal{X}})\right)

\displaystyle+\sum_{i\neq j}\sum_{\mathcal{A}\in\lambda_{i,v}}\sum_{\mathcal{B}\in\lambda_{j,v}}h^{0}(\mathcal{O}_{p_{v}}(\mathcal{A}-\mathcal{B}+\mathcal{D}_{j,v})),

where
\ell(\widetilde{\pi}_{v})+\|\overline{\pi}_{v}\|=\sum_{\mathcal{A}\in\breve{\pi}_{v}}\zeta_{\breve{\pi}_{v}}(\mathcal{A})
. See also [
45
, Page 94].

Remark 3.20
.

When
\mathcal{X}
is a scheme
X
, then
\mathcal{C}_{e}\cong\mathbb{P}^{1}
for all
e\in E_{\mathcal{X}}
. By Remark
3.19
and [
7
, Example 22], we have

\displaystyle s_{2}(\mathbf{E}(e))=m_{e}|\lambda_{e}|\;\mathrm{mod}\;2

and

\displaystyle s_{2}(\mathbf{V}(v))

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle s_{2}\left(\ell(\widetilde{\pi}_{v})+\sum_{\mathcal{D}\in\widetilde{\pi}_{v}}\eta_{\widetilde{\pi}_{v}}(\mathcal{D})+3\sum_{\mathcal{A},\mathcal{B}\in\widetilde{\pi}_{v}}\eta_{\widetilde{\pi}_{v}}(\mathcal{A})\eta_{\widetilde{\pi}_{v}}(\mathcal{B})+6\sum_{\mathcal{A}\in\widetilde{\pi}_{v}}\sum_{\mathcal{B}\in\overline{\pi}_{v}}\eta_{\widetilde{\pi}_{v}}(\mathcal{A})\xi_{\overline{\pi}_{v}}(\mathcal{B})\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle s_{2}\left(2\ell(\widetilde{\pi}_{v})+3\ell(\widetilde{\pi}_{v})^{2}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\ell(\widetilde{\pi}_{v})\;\mathrm{mod}\;2.

Then

\displaystyle s_{1}(\mathrm{Ext}^{1}(\mathbf{I}^{\bullet},\mathbf{I}^{\bullet})_{0})=s_{2}\left(\chi(\mathcal{O}_{\mathcal{C}_{\mathcal{F}}})+\sum_{v\in V_{\mathcal{X}}}\ell(\widetilde{\pi}_{v})+\sum_{e\in E_{\mathcal{X}}}m_{e}|\lambda_{e}|\right)

exactly coincides with the sign in [
34
, Theorem/Conjecture 2] proved in [
14
] due to

\displaystyle\chi(\mathcal{F})=\chi(\mathcal{O}_{\mathcal{C}_{\mathcal{F}}})+\chi(\mathtt{Q})=\chi(\mathcal{O}_{\mathcal{C}_{\mathcal{F}}})+\sum_{v\in V_{\mathcal{X}}}\ell(\widetilde{\pi}_{v}).

Assume that the local model of a torus fixed point
p_{v}
is of the form
[\mathbb{C}^{3}/G_{v}]
with a finite abelian group
G_{v}
. Let
r_{i}\in\widehat{G_{v}}

be the character obtained from the action of
G_{v}
on
\mathcal{O}_{p_{v}}(-D_{i})
for
1\leq i\leq 3
, different from the convention in [
7
, Example 23]. Let
0\in\widehat{G_{v}}
be the trivial character where we have identified the group
\widehat{G_{v}}
of characters of
G_{v}\subset(\mathbb{C}^{*})^{3}
with the corresponding additive subgroup of
\mathbb{Z}^{3}
. Then
we have

\displaystyle s_{2}(\mathbf{V}(v))

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle s_{2}(\ell(\widetilde{\pi}_{v})_{0}+\ell(\widetilde{\pi}_{v})+\|\overline{\pi}_{v}\|)+\sum_{i\neq j}\sum_{r\in\widehat{G_{v}}}s_{2}(|\lambda_{i,v}|_{r}\cdot|\lambda_{j,v}|_{r+r_{j}})

\displaystyle+\sum_{r\in\widehat{G_{v}}}s_{2}\bigg((\ell(\widetilde{\pi}_{v})_{r}+\|\overline{\pi}_{v}\|_{r})\sum_{i=1}^{3}(\ell(\widetilde{\pi}_{v})_{r+r_{i}}+\|\overline{\pi}_{v}\|_{r+r_{i}})\bigg)

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=1}^{3}\sum_{r\in\widehat{G_{v}}}s_{2}\bigg(|\lambda_{i,v}|_{r}\bigg(\ell(\widetilde{\pi}_{v})_{r}+\|\overline{\pi}_{v}\|_{r}+\sum_{j\in\{1,2,3\}\setminus\{i\}}(\ell(\widetilde{\pi}_{v})_{r+r_{j}}+\|\overline{\pi}_{v}\|_{r+r_{j}})

\displaystyle+\ell(\widetilde{\pi}_{v})_{r-r_{i}+\sum_{j=1}^{3}r_{j}}+\|\overline{\pi}_{v}\|_{r-r_{i}+\sum_{j=1}^{3}r_{j}}\bigg)\bigg)

where
|\lambda_{i,v}|_{r}
is the number of elements in 2D partition
\lambda_{i,v}\subset\mathbb{Z}^{2}
colored by
r\in\widehat{G_{v}}
, and
\ell(\widetilde{\pi}_{v})_{r}
,
\|\overline{\pi}_{v}\|_{r}
are defined as in Section 3.1.

As in the DT case [
7
, Remark 24], one can obtain the general formula for the PT partition function
PT(\mathcal{X})
in Definition
3.17
by combining Lemma
3.13
, Proposition
3.15
, and Theorem
3.18
with the formula (
6
). The explicit formula is presented in the following transverse
A_{n-1}
case.

3.4.
The PT topological vertex formalism

Let
\mathcal{X}
be a toric CY 3-orbifold with transverse
A_{n-1}
singularities. We present the explicit formula for PT partition function of
\mathcal{X}
for full 3-leg case as follows.
We first recall some notations in [
7
, Section 3.3] that are used later.
For a 2D ordinary partition
\lambda
, define

\displaystyle\lambda[k,n]:=\{(i,j)\in\lambda\;|\;i-j\equiv k\mbox{ mod }n\}\;\;\;\mbox{and}\;\;\;|\lambda|_{k,n}:=|\lambda[k,n]|

where we also write
|\lambda|_{k,n}
as
|\lambda|_{k}
for simplicity. We call a partition
\eta
multi-regular if
|\eta|_{k}=\frac{|\eta|}{n}
for all
k\in\{0,1,\cdots,n-1\}
. With some abuse of notation, we use the notation
|\cdot|_{k}
not only for edge partitions
\lambda_{e}
with the above definition, but also for outgoing partitions
(\lambda_{1,v},\lambda_{2,v},\lambda_{3,v})
of labelled box configurations
\widetilde{\pi}_{v}
with the slightly different convention: we define the color of
(j,k)\in\lambda_{1,v}
,
(k,i)\in\lambda_{2,v}
, and
(i,j)\in\lambda_{3,v}
by
-j\mbox{ mod }n
,
i\mbox{ mod }n
, and
i-j\mbox{ mod }n
respectively due to transverse
A_{n-1}
singularities and hence
|\lambda_{i,v}|_{l}
is defined to be the number of boxes in
\lambda_{i,v}
colored by
l\mbox{ mod }n
for
1\leq i\leq 3
where
n=n_{e_{3,v}}
. Define

\displaystyle\varXi_{\widetilde{\pi}_{v}}=\sum_{k=0}^{n_{e_{3,v}}-1}|\lambda_{3,v}|_{k}(|\lambda_{1,v}|_{k}+|\lambda_{2,v}|_{k}+|\lambda_{1,v}|_{k+1}+|\lambda_{2,v}|_{k-1}).

Let

\displaystyle C_{m,m^{\prime}}^{\lambda}:=\sum_{(i,j)\in\lambda}\left(-mi-m^{\prime}j+1\right),

\displaystyle C_{m,m^{\prime}}^{\lambda}[k,n]:=\sum_{(i,j)\in\lambda[k,n]}\left(-mi-m^{\prime}j+1\right),

\displaystyle A_{\lambda}(k,n):=\sum_{(i,j)\in\lambda}\left\lfloor\frac{i+k}{n}\right\rfloor,

and

\displaystyle q_{e}^{C_{m_{e},m_{e}^{\prime}}^{\lambda_{e}}}=:\prod_{k=0}^{n_{e}-1}q_{e,k}^{C_{m_{e},m_{e}^{\prime}}^{\lambda_{e}}[k,n_{e}]},\;\;\;\;\;q_{e}^{A_{\lambda}}:=\prod_{k=0}^{n_{e}-1}q_{e,k}^{A_{\lambda}(k,n_{e})}.

We define for each edge
e\in E_{\mathcal{X}}

\displaystyle\mathbf{q}_{e}=(q_{e,0},q_{e,1},\cdots,q_{e,n_{e}-1}),\;\;\;\overline{\mathbf{q}_{e}}=(q_{e,0},q_{e,n_{e}-1},\cdots,q_{e,1})

and

\displaystyle\mathbf{S}_{\lambda_{e}}^{e}=\sum_{k=0}^{n_{e}-1}\bigg(C_{m_{e},m^{\prime}_{e}}^{\lambda_{e}}[k,n_{e}](|\lambda_{e}|_{k-1}-|\lambda_{e}|_{k+1})+|\lambda_{e}|_{k}\big(1+(1+m_{e}+\delta_{0,e}+\delta_{\infty,e})|\lambda_{e}|_{k-1}\big)\bigg).

Let

\displaystyle\mathbf{q}_{v}=\left\{\begin{aligned} &\mathbf{q}_{e_{3,v}},\;\;\;\mbox{if $e_{3,v}$ is oriented outward from $v$};\\
&\overline{\mathbf{q}_{e_{3,v}}},\;\;\;\mbox{if $e_{3,v}$ is oriented inward toward $v$}.\end{aligned}\right.

and
(-1)^{\widetilde{s}_{k}(\lambda_{3,v})}\mathbf{q}_{v}
be the vector
\mathbf{q}_{v}
with its each component
q_{e,k}
multiplied by the sign
(-1)^{\widetilde{s}_{k}(\lambda_{3,v})}
where
\widetilde{s}_{k}(\lambda_{3,v})=|\lambda_{3,v}|_{k-1}+|\lambda_{3,v}|_{k+1}
and
e=e_{3,v}
.

With these notations, we have

Theorem 3.21
.

Let
\mathcal{X}
be a toric CY 3-orbifold with transverse
A_{n-1}
-singularities. Then the pairity of
\dim\mathrm{Ext}(\mathbf{I}^{\bullet},\mathbf{I}^{\bullet})_{0}
in Theorem
3.18
is

\displaystyle s_{1}(\mathrm{Ext}^{1}(\mathbf{I}^{\bullet},\mathbf{I}^{\bullet})_{0})=s_{2}(\chi(\mathcal{O}_{\mathcal{C}_{\mathcal{F}}}))+\sum_{v\in V_{\mathcal{X}}}s_{2}(\mathbf{V}(v))+\sum_{e\in E_{\mathcal{X}}}s_{2}(\mathbf{E}(e))

where

\displaystyle\mathbf{E}(e)=\left\{\begin{aligned} &|\lambda_{e}|(m_{e}+\delta_{0}+\delta_{\infty}),\;\;\;\mbox{if}\;\;n_{e}=1;\\
&\sum_{k=0}^{n-1}C_{m_{e},m^{\prime}_{e}}^{\lambda_{e}}[k,n](|\lambda_{e}|_{k-1}-|\lambda_{e}|_{k+1})+|\lambda_{e}|_{k}(1+(1+m_{e})|\lambda_{e}|_{k-1}),\;\;\mbox{if}\;\;n_{e}>1.\end{aligned}\right.

and

\displaystyle\mathbf{V}(v)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\ell(\widetilde{\pi}_{v})_{0}+\sum_{k=0}^{n_{e_{3,v}}-1}(\ell(\widetilde{\pi}_{v})_{k}+\|\overline{\pi}_{v}\|_{k})(|\lambda_{3,v}|_{k-1}+|\lambda_{3,v}|_{k+1})

\displaystyle+\sum_{k=0}^{n_{e_{{3,v}}}-1}|\lambda_{3,v}|_{k}(|\lambda_{1,v}|_{k}+|\lambda_{2,v}|_{k}+|\lambda_{1,v}|_{k+1}+|\lambda_{2,v}|_{k-1}).

Proof.

It follows from Theorem
3.18
and the similar argument in the proof of [
7
, Theorem 25]. See also the proof of [
45
, Proposition 4.29].
∎

Let
\Gamma_{\mathcal{X}}
be the web diagram of
\mathcal{X}
. Given an edge assignment
\{\lambda_{e}\;|\;e\in E_{\mathcal{X}}\}\in\Lambda_{\mathcal{X}}
, there should be many collections of labelled box configurations where each collection
\{\widetilde{\pi}_{v}\;|\;v\in V_{\mathcal{X}}\}
of labelled box configurations
\widetilde{\pi}_{v}
with outgoing partitions
(\lambda_{1,v},\lambda_{2,v},\lambda_{3,v})
determined by
\{\lambda_{e}\}
via the compatibility in Section 2.1. And each set
\{\widetilde{\pi}_{v},\lambda_{e}\}
corresponds to one
T
-fixed point in
\coprod_{\beta\in F_{1}K(\mathcal{X})}\mathrm{PT}(\mathcal{X},\beta)
by Lemma
3.13
. Therefore, for a given edge assignment
\{\lambda_{e}\}\in\Lambda_{\mathcal{X}}
, there are corresponding several connected components of
T
-fixed locus of
\coprod\limits_{\beta\in F_{1}K(\mathcal{X})}\mathrm{PT}(\mathcal{X},\beta)
where each component is of the form
\prod\limits_{v\in V_{\mathcal{X}}}[\widetilde{\pi}_{v}]
satisfying some compatible conditions.

Now we have the following PT version of [

, Theorem 10], see also [

, Theorem/Conjecture 4.19].

Theorem 3.22
.

Let
\mathcal{X}
be a toric CY 3-orbifold with transverse
A_{n-1}
singularities. Define

\displaystyle\underline{PT}(\mathcal{X}):=\sum_{\{\lambda_{e}\}\in\Lambda_{\mathcal{X}}}\prod_{e\in E_{\mathcal{X}}}E_{\lambda_{e}}^{e}\prod_{v\in V_{\mathcal{X}}}(-1)^{\varXi_{\widetilde{\pi}_{v}}}W_{\lambda_{1,v}\lambda_{2,v}\lambda_{3,v}}^{n_{e_{3,v}}}((-1)^{\widetilde{s}(\lambda_{3,v})}\mathbf{q}_{v})

where

\displaystyle E_{\lambda_{e}}^{e}=(-1)^{\mathbf{S}_{\lambda_{e}}^{e}}\cdot\left(\prod_{k=0}^{n_{e}-1}v_{e,k}^{|\lambda_{e}|_{k,n_{e}}}\right)\cdot q_{e}^{C_{m_{e},m_{e}^{\prime}}^{\lambda_{e}}}\cdot\left(\overline{q_{f_{v_{0}}}^{A_{\lambda_{e}}}}\right)^{\delta_{0,e}}\cdot\left(q_{f^{\prime}_{v_{0}}}^{A_{\lambda^{\prime}_{e}}}\right)^{\delta_{0,e}^{\prime}}\cdot\left(q_{g_{v_{\infty}}}^{A_{\lambda_{e}}}\right)^{\delta_{\infty,e}}\cdot\left(\overline{q_{g^{\prime}_{v_{\infty}}}^{A_{\lambda^{\prime}_{e}}}}\right)^{\delta_{\infty,e}^{\prime}}

with edges
(e,f_{v_{0}},f^{\prime}_{v_{0}},g_{v_{\infty}},g^{\prime}_{v_{\infty}})
are the oriented edges presented as in Figure
1
, and the overline denotes the exchange of variables
q_{k}\leftrightarrow q_{-k}
.
Then we have

\displaystyle PT(\mathcal{X})=\underline{PT}(\mathcal{X})\bigg|_{q_{e,0}\to-q_{e,0},\;q\to-q}

that is,
PT(\mathcal{X})
is obtained from
\underline{PT}(\mathcal{X})
by replacing varibles
q_{e,0}
by
-q_{e,0}
for all
e\in E_{\mathcal{X}}
and hence replacing
q
by
-q
.

Proof.

Since

\displaystyle\mathcal{O}_{\mathcal{C}_{\mathcal{F}}}=\sum_{v\in V_{\mathcal{X}}}\sum_{\mathcal{D}\in\overline{\pi}_{v}}\xi_{\overline{\pi}_{v}}(\mathcal{D})\mathcal{O}_{p_{v}}(-\mathcal{D})+\sum_{e\in E_{\mathcal{X}}}\sum_{\mathcal{D}\in\lambda_{e}}\mathcal{O}_{\mathcal{C}_{e}}(-\mathcal{D})

then

\displaystyle\chi(\mathcal{O}_{\mathcal{C}_{\mathcal{F}}})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{v\in V_{\mathcal{X}}}\|\overline{\pi}_{v}\|_{0}+\sum_{e\in E_{\mathcal{X}}}C_{m_{e},m_{e}^{\prime}}^{\lambda_{e}}[0,n_{e}]+\sum_{e\in E_{\mathcal{X}}}\bigg(\delta_{0,e}\cdot A_{\lambda_{e}}(0,n_{f_{v_{0}}})

\displaystyle+\delta_{0,e}^{\prime}\cdot A_{\lambda_{e}^{\prime}}(0,n_{f^{\prime}_{v_{0}}})+\delta_{\infty,e}\cdot A_{\lambda_{e}}(0,n_{g_{v_{\infty}}})+\delta^{\prime}_{\infty,e}\cdot A_{\lambda^{\prime}_{e}}(0,n_{g^{\prime}_{v_{\infty}}})\bigg).

The proof is completed by using Definitions
3.10
,
3.17
, the formula (
6
), Proposition
3.15
for
\beta=\mathcal{F}
, Theorem
3.21
, and [
7
, Lemma 15 and Proposition 5] via the similar argument in [
7
, Section 6.3]. ∎

Remark 3.23
.

The terms involving
f_{v_{0}}
and
g^{\prime}_{v_{\infty}}
in the expression of
E_{\lambda_{e}}^{e}
are added by the overline since the choice of orientations of
f_{v_{0}}
and
g^{\prime}_{v_{\infty}}
opposite to those of
f
and
g^{\prime}
in [
7
] exchanges generators
[\mathcal{O}_{p_{f_{v_{0}}}}\otimes\rho_{k}]\leftrightarrow[\mathcal{O}_{p_{f_{v_{0}}}}\otimes\rho_{-k}]
,
[\mathcal{O}_{p_{g^{\prime}_{v_{\infty}}}}\otimes\rho_{k}]\leftrightarrow[\mathcal{O}_{p_{g^{\prime}_{v_{\infty}}}}\otimes\rho_{-k}]
in [
7
, Lemma 17 and Proposition 5].

4.
Orbifold DT theory and the graphical condensation recurrence

In this section, we show the dimer model for DT theory in [
14
, Section 3.2] can be extended to our orbifold case in Section 4.1 and generalize the graphical condensation recurrence for DT theory in [
14
, Section 3.3] in Section 4.2, where we also obtain two more recurrences for orbifold DT theory and exhibit the equivalence between them by the symmetry of DT
\mathbb{Z}_{n}
-vertex. We compute the weights appearing in these three recurrences in Section 4.3, which generalize the ones in [
14
, Section 5.2].

4.1.
The dimer model for orbifold DT theory

In order to describe the orbifold DT topological vertex as some dimer model, we recall some relevant notation and definitions in [
14
, Section 2 and 3] as follows. Notice that any ordinary 2D partition
\eta
in this section has its parts indexed starting from 1 other than 0 in Section 3.

Definition 4.1
.

([
14
, Definition 2.0.1 and 2.0.2])
For any partition
\eta=(\eta_{1},\eta_{2},\cdots,\eta_{l})
, define the Maya diagram of
\eta
by the set

\displaystyle S(\eta)=\left\{\eta_{i}-i+\frac{1}{2}\;\bigg|\;i\geq 1\right\}\subseteq\mathbb{Z}+\frac{1}{2}.

For a subset
S\subseteq\mathbb{Z}+\frac{1}{2}
, define

\displaystyle S^{+}=\left\{t\in S\;\bigg|\;t>0\right\};\;\;\;\;\;S^{-}=\left\{t\in\mathbb{Z}+\frac{1}{2}\setminus S\;\bigg|\;t<0\right\}.

If
|S^{+}|,|S^{-}|<+\infty
, define the charge of
S
as

\displaystyle c(S)=|S^{+}|-|S^{-}|.

Then the set
\{t-c(S)\;|\;t\in S\}
is the Maya diagram of some partition
\widehat{\eta}
, i.e.,

\displaystyle\{t-c(S)\;|\;t\in S\}=S(\widehat{\eta})

and we call
S
the charge
c(S)
Maya diagram of
\widehat{\eta}
.

Definition 4.2
.

([
14
, Definition 2.0.3])
For any partition
\eta\neq\emptyset
with the Maya diagram
S(\eta)
, the partitions
\eta^{r}
,
\eta^{c}
and
\eta^{rc}
are defined by the following equations respectively

\displaystyle S(\eta^{r})=\{t+1\;|\;t\in S(\eta)\setminus\{\min S(\eta)^{+}\}\},

\displaystyle S(\eta^{c})=\{t-1\;|\;t\in S(\eta)\cup\{\max S(\eta)^{-}\}\},

\displaystyle S(\eta^{rc})=\{t\;|\;t\in(S(\eta)\setminus\{\min S(\eta)^{+}\})\cup\{\max S(\eta)^{-}\}\}.

That is, the set
S(\eta)\setminus\{\min S(\eta)^{+}\}
is the charge
-1
Maya diagram of
\eta^{r}
, the set
S(\eta)\cup\{\max S(\eta)^{-}\}
is the charge
1
Maya diagram of
\eta^{c}
, and the set
(S(\eta)\setminus\{\min S(\eta)^{+}\})\cup\{\max S(\eta)^{-}\}
is the (charge
0
) Maya diagram of
\eta^{rc}
.

As in [
14
], let
\mathbf{H}(N)
be the
N\times N\times N
honeycomb graph divided into three sectors with some choice of labellings of vertices on the outer face, which is presented in different ways for the DT and PT case respectively, see Figure
2
for the graph
\mathbf{H}(4)
. With the convention in [
14
], the labelled vertice
v
in sector
i
is said to be in sector
i^{+}
or
i^{-}
if the labelled number is positive or negative.

Figure 2.
The left graph
\mathbf{H}(4)
is divided into 3 sectors with the labellings of outer vertices for DT case. The right graph is again
\mathbf{H}(4)
but divided into another 3 sectors with the different labellings of outer vertices for PT case.

As is shown in [
16
,
14
], a 3D partition (or a plane partition) visualized as some collection of boxes can be viewed from a suitable direction as certain rhombus tiling within a hexagonal region consisting of triangles, denoted by faces of a finite planar graph. And its dual graph is exactly the beautiful honeycomb graph, which is the dimer configuration (or the so-called perfect matching) of this dual graph, see [
16
, Fig. 17] or [
14
, Figure 3] for examples. Labelled box configurations are related to AB configurations which also have this visualized description in Section 5.2. In order to describe the orbifold DT or PT topological vertex formula via some weighted configurations on the modified honeycomb graph as in [
14
], we assign the weights of edges of the graph
\mathbf{H}(N)
as follows.

Definition 4.3
.

Given
n\in\mathbb{Z}_{>0}
, the honeycomb graph
\mathbf{H}(N)
is weighted by the following rule:

(i)
all the non-horizontal edges are weighted by 1;

(ii)
the
2N
horizontal edges in the central column are weighted from bottom to top by

\displaystyle q_{0}^{0},q_{0}^{1},q_{0}^{2},\cdots,q_{0}^{2N-1};

(iii)
for
1\leq i\leq N-1
, the
(2N-i)
horizontal edges in the
i
-th column on the right hand side of the central column (corresponding to
0
-th column) are weighted from bottom to top by

\displaystyle q_{-i}^{0},q_{-i}^{1},q_{-i}^{2},\cdots,q_{-i}^{2N-i-1};

(iv)
for
1\leq i\leq N-1
, the
(2N-i)
horizontal edges in the
i
-th column on the left hand side of the central column (corresponding to
0
-th column) are weighted from bottom to top by

\displaystyle q_{i}^{i},q_{i}^{i+1},q_{i}^{i+2},\cdots,q_{i}^{2N-1},

where for any
k\in\mathbb{Z}
, the equality
q_{k}=q_{k^{\prime}}
is used implicitly through this paper if
k^{\prime}\in\{0,1,\cdots,n-1\}
and
k^{\prime}\equiv k\mbox{ mod }n
.

The weighted rule in Definition
4.3
is shown in Figure
3
for
\mathbf{H}(4)
as an example.

Remark 4.4
.

The weighted rule is defined such that if one add a box
(i,j,k)
to a 3D partition asymptotic to
(\lambda,\mu,\nu)
, the weight of the corresponding dimer configuration should be multiplied by a factor of
q_{i-j}
, and removing a box
(i,j,k)
will multiply the weight by a factor
q_{i-j}^{-1}
. If all
q_{l}
are equal to
q
, the weighted rule here generalizes the one in [
16
, Section 6] (see also [
14
, Definition 2.0.4]).

Let
(\lambda,\mu,\nu)
be a triple of partitions with their Maya diagrams

\displaystyle S_{1}:=S(\lambda),\;\;\;\;S_{2}:=S(\mu),\;\;\;\;S_{3}:=S(\nu).

The modified honeycomb graph
\mathbf{H}(N;\lambda,\mu,\nu)
is defined by removing vertices labelled by elements in
S_{i}^{+}\cup S_{i}^{-}
from the
i
-th sector of
\mathbf{H}(N)
.
If we view any 3D partition asymptotic to
(\lambda,\mu,\nu)
as a collection of boxes, it is shown in [
14
, Section 3.2] that there is a bijective correspondence between all these box collections and all the dimer configurations on (or perfect matchings of)
\mathbf{H}(N;\lambda,\mu,\nu)
. One may refer to [
14
, Figure 3] as an example for this correspondence.

Let
\mathbf{Z}^{D}(\mathbf{G})
be the sum of the weights of all dimer configurations on the graph
\mathbf{G}
. There is a unique minimal dimer configuration on
\mathbf{H}(N;\lambda,\mu,\nu)
corresponding to the 3D partition
\pi_{\min}(\lambda,\mu,\nu)
defined in Section 3. By the weighted rule, the dimer configuration being minimal here means that if we set all
q_{l}
to be equal to
q
, the weight of this dimer configuration with respect to
q
is minimal as in [
14
], or that this dimer configuration has minimal weight with respect to each
q_{l}
. Denote this minimal dimer configuration by
\mathbf{D}_{\min}(\lambda,\mu,\nu;N)
and its weight by
q^{\omega_{\min}(\lambda,\mu,\nu;N)}
, which is a polynomial of variables
q_{1-N},q_{2-N},\cdots,q_{0},\cdots,q_{N-2},q_{N-1}
. For any subset
\mathbf{S}\subseteq\mathbb{Z}^{3}
, we
define

\displaystyle|\mathbf{S}|_{l}=\{(i,j,k)\in\mathbf{S}\;|\;i-j\equiv l\mbox{ mod }n\}.

Then we have

\displaystyle\lim_{N\to\infty}\left(q^{\omega_{\min}(\lambda,\mu,\nu;N)}\right)^{-1}\cdot\mathbf{Z}^{D}(\mathbf{H}(N;\lambda,\mu,\nu))=V^{n}_{\lambda\mu\nu}\cdot\prod_{l=0}^{n-1}q_{l}^{|\mathrm{II}(\lambda,\mu,\nu)|_{l}}\cdot q_{l}^{2|\mathrm{III}(\lambda,\mu,\nu)|_{l}}.

This implies the following orbifold version of [
14
, Theorem 3.2.1].

Theorem 4.5
.

We have the following limit in the sense of formal Laurent series

\displaystyle\lim_{N\to\infty}\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}^{D}(\mathbf{H}(N;\lambda,\mu,\nu))=V^{n}_{\lambda\mu\nu}(q_{0},q_{1},\cdots,q_{n-1})

where

\displaystyle\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}^{D}(\mathbf{H}(N;\lambda,\mu,\nu)):=\left(q^{\widetilde{\omega}_{\min}(\lambda,\mu,\nu;N)}\right)^{-1}\cdot\mathbf{Z}^{D}(\mathbf{H}(N;\lambda,\mu,\nu))

and

\displaystyle q^{\widetilde{\omega}_{\min}(\lambda,\mu,\nu;N)}:=q^{\omega_{\min}(\lambda,\mu,\nu;N)}\cdot\prod\limits_{l=0}^{n-1}q_{l}^{|\mathrm{II}(\lambda,\mu,\nu)|_{l}}\cdot q_{l}^{2|\mathrm{III}(\lambda,\mu,\nu)|_{l}}.

4.2.
The graphical condensation recurrence for orbifold DT theory

The graphical condensation is discovered by Kuo in [
16
], and is successfully applied in [
14
] to derive the recurrence for DT topological vertex. In this section, we will obtain graphical condensation recurrences for DT
\mathbb{Z}_{n}
-vertex with the weighted rule in Definition
4.3
by the following technique.

Theorem 4.6
.

([
16
, Theorem 5.1])
Let
\mathbf{G}=(\mathbf{V}_{1},\mathbf{V}_{2},\mathbf{E})
be a given edge-weighted plane bipartite graph with
|\mathbf{V}_{1}|=|\mathbf{V}_{2}|
. Let vertices
a,b,c
and
d
appear on a face of
\mathbf{G}
in a cyclic order. If
a,c\in\mathbf{V}_{1}
and
b,d\in\mathbf{V}_{2}
, then

\displaystyle\mathbf{Z}^{D}(\mathbf{G})\mathbf{Z}^{D}(\mathbf{G}-\{a,b,c,d\})=\mathbf{Z}^{D}(\mathbf{G}-\{a,b\})\mathbf{Z}^{D}(\mathbf{G}-\{c,d\})+\mathbf{Z}^{D}(\mathbf{G}-\{a,d\})\mathbf{Z}^{D}(\mathbf{G}-\{b,c\}).

As in [
14
], we choose
\mathbf{G}
to be
\mathbf{H}(N;\lambda^{rc},\mu^{rc},\nu)
and let

\displaystyle a:=\max S_{1}^{-},\;\;\;b:=\min S_{1}^{+},\;\;\;c=\max S_{2}^{-},\;\;\;d=\min S_{2}^{+}.

Then we have

\displaystyle\mathbf{G}-\{a,b,c,d\}=\mathbf{H}(N;\lambda,\mu,\nu),

\displaystyle\mathbf{G}-\{a,b\}=\mathbf{H}(N;\lambda,\mu^{rc},\nu),

\displaystyle\mathbf{G}-\{c,d\}=\mathbf{H}(N;\lambda^{rc},\mu,\nu).

It is an important observation [
14
] that the graph
\mathbf{G}-\{a,d\}
is derived from the charge
-1
Maya diagram of
\lambda^{r}
, the charge 1 Maya diagram of
\mu^{c}
, and (the charge 0) Maya diagram of
\nu
, and that 3D partitions asymptotic to
(\lambda^{r},\mu^{c},\nu)
are corresponding to dimer configurations on
\mathbf{G}-\{a,d\}
but with the orgin in
\mathbb{Z}^{3}
shifted to the face directly above the central face of
\mathbf{H}(N)
. While the graph
\mathbf{G}-\{b,c\}
is obtained from the charge 1 Maya diagram of
\lambda^{c}
, the charge
-1
Maya diagram of
\mu^{r}
, and Maya diagram of
\nu
, together with 3D partitions asymptotic to
(\lambda^{c},\mu^{r},\nu)
corresponding to dimer configurations on
\mathbf{G}-\{b,c\}
but with the orgin in
\mathbb{Z}^{3}
shifted to the face directly below the central face of
\mathbf{H}(N)
. One can refer to [
14
, Figure 4 and 5] for example. Let
\mathbf{D}_{\min}^{\uparrow}(\lambda^{r},\mu^{c},\nu;N)
and
\mathbf{D}_{\min}^{\downarrow}(\lambda^{c},\mu^{r},\nu;N)
be the minimal dimer configurations on
\mathbf{G}-\{a,d\}
and
\mathbf{G}-\{b,c\}
with their weights denoted by
q^{\omega_{\min}^{U}(\lambda^{r},\mu^{c},\nu;N)}
and
q^{\omega_{\min}^{D}(\lambda^{c},\mu^{r},\nu;N)}
respectively.
Let

\displaystyle q^{\widetilde{\omega}_{\min}^{U}(\lambda^{r},\mu^{c},\nu;N)}=q^{\omega_{\min}^{U}(\lambda^{r},\mu^{c},\nu;N)}\cdot\prod\limits_{l=0}^{n-1}q_{l}^{|\mathrm{II}(\lambda^{r},\mu^{c},\nu)|_{l}}\cdot q_{l}^{2|\mathrm{III}(\lambda^{r},\mu^{c},\nu)|_{l}},

\displaystyle q^{\widetilde{\omega}_{\min}^{D}}(\lambda^{c},\mu^{r},\nu;N)=q^{\omega_{\min}^{D}(\lambda^{c},\mu^{r},\nu;N)}\cdot\prod\limits_{l=0}^{n-1}q_{l}^{|\mathrm{II}(\lambda^{c},\mu^{r},\nu)|_{l}}\cdot q_{l}^{2|\mathrm{III}(\lambda^{c},\mu^{r},\nu)|_{l}}.

Set

\displaystyle\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}^{D}(\mathbf{H}(N;\lambda^{rc},\mu^{rc},\nu)-\{a,d\}):=\left(q^{\widetilde{\omega}_{\min}^{U}(\lambda^{r},\mu^{c},\nu;N)}\right)^{-1}\cdot\mathbf{Z}^{D}(\mathbf{H}(N;\lambda^{rc},\mu^{rc},\nu)-\{a,d\}),

\displaystyle\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}^{D}(\mathbf{H}(N;\lambda^{rc},\mu^{rc},\nu)-\{b,c\}):=\left(q^{\widetilde{\omega}_{\min}^{D}(\lambda^{c},\mu^{r},\nu;N)}\right)^{-1}\cdot\mathbf{Z}^{D}(\mathbf{H}(N;\lambda^{rc},\mu^{rc},\nu)-\{b,c\}).

As in Section 4.1, we have

\displaystyle\lim_{N\to\infty}\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}^{D}(\mathbf{H}(N;\lambda^{rc},\mu^{rc},\nu)-\{a,d\})=V^{n}_{\lambda^{r}\mu^{c}\nu},

\displaystyle\lim_{N\to\infty}\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}^{D}(\mathbf{H}(N;\lambda^{rc},\mu^{rc},\nu)-\{b,c\})=V^{n}_{\lambda^{c}\mu^{r}\nu}.

Now it follows from Theorem
4.6

that

\displaystyle q^{\widetilde{\omega}_{\min}(\lambda,\mu,\nu;N)}\cdot q^{\widetilde{\omega}_{\min}(\lambda^{rc},\mu^{rc},\nu;N)}\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}^{D}(\mathbf{H}(N;\lambda,\mu,\nu))\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}^{D}(\mathbf{H}(N;\lambda^{rc},\mu^{rc},\nu))

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle q^{\widetilde{\omega}_{\min}(\lambda^{rc},\mu,\nu;N)}\cdot q^{\widetilde{\omega}_{\min}(\lambda,\mu^{rc},\nu;N)}\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}^{D}(\mathbf{H}(N;\lambda^{rc},\mu,\nu))\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}^{D}(\mathbf{H}(N;\lambda,\mu^{rc},\nu))

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle q^{\widetilde{\omega}_{\min}^{U}(\lambda^{r},\mu^{c},\nu;N)}\cdot q^{\widetilde{\omega}_{\min}^{D}(\lambda^{c},\mu^{r},\nu;N)}\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}^{D}(\mathbf{H}(N;\lambda^{rc},\mu^{rc},\nu)-\{a,d\})\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}^{D}(\mathbf{H}(N;\lambda^{rc},\mu^{rc},\nu)-\{b,c\}).

Then

\displaystyle\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}^{D}(\mathbf{H}(N;\lambda,\mu,\nu))\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}^{D}(\mathbf{H}(N;\lambda^{rc},\mu^{rc},\nu))

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{q^{\widetilde{\omega}_{\min}(\lambda^{rc},\mu,\nu;N)}\cdot q^{\widetilde{\omega}_{\min}(\lambda,\mu^{rc},\nu;N)}}{q^{\widetilde{\omega}_{\min}(\lambda,\mu,\nu;N)}\cdot q^{\widetilde{\omega}_{\min}(\lambda^{rc},\mu^{rc},\nu;N)}}\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}^{D}(\mathbf{H}(N;\lambda^{rc},\mu,\nu))\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}^{D}(\mathbf{H}(N;\lambda,\mu^{rc},\nu))

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\frac{q^{\widetilde{\omega}_{\min}^{U}(\lambda^{r},\mu^{c},\nu;N)}\cdot q^{\widetilde{\omega}_{\min}^{D}(\lambda^{c},\mu^{r},\nu;N)}}{q^{\widetilde{\omega}_{\min}(\lambda,\mu,\nu;N)}\cdot q^{\widetilde{\omega}_{\min}(\lambda^{rc},\mu^{rc},\nu;N)}}\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}^{D}(\mathbf{H}(N;\lambda^{rc},\mu^{rc},\nu)-\{a,d\})\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}^{D}(\mathbf{H}(N;\lambda^{rc},\mu^{rc},\nu)-\{b,c\}).

It is proved in Section 4.3.1 that

\displaystyle\frac{q^{\widetilde{\omega}_{\min}(\lambda^{rc},\mu,\nu;N)}\cdot q^{\widetilde{\omega}_{\min}(\lambda,\mu^{rc},\nu;N)}}{q^{\widetilde{\omega}_{\min}(\lambda,\mu,\nu;N)}\cdot q^{\widetilde{\omega}_{\min}(\lambda^{rc},\mu^{rc},\nu;N)}}=1\;\;\;\;\;\;\mbox{and}\;\;\;\;\;\;\frac{q^{\widetilde{\omega}_{\min}^{U}(\lambda^{r},\mu^{c},\nu;N)}\cdot q^{\widetilde{\omega}_{\min}^{D}(\lambda^{c},\mu^{r},\nu;N)}}{q^{\widetilde{\omega}_{\min}(\lambda,\mu,\nu;N)}\cdot q^{\widetilde{\omega}_{\min}(\lambda^{rc},\mu^{rc},\nu;N)}}=q^{K_{1}(\lambda,\mu,\nu)}

where
q^{K_{1}(\lambda,\mu,\nu)}

is defined in Lemma
4.17
and independent of
N
. Now take
N\to\infty
, we have the following graphical condensation recurrence

\displaystyle V^{n}_{\lambda\mu\nu}\cdot V^{n}_{\lambda^{rc}\mu^{rc}\nu}=V^{n}_{\lambda^{rc}\mu\nu}\cdot V^{n}_{\lambda\mu^{rc}\nu}+q^{K_{1}(\lambda,\mu,\nu)}\cdot V^{n}_{\lambda^{r}\mu^{c}\nu}\cdot V^{n}_{\lambda^{c}\mu^{r}\nu}.

Then we have

(7)

\displaystyle\frac{V^{n}_{\lambda\mu\nu}}{V^{n}_{\emptyset\emptyset\emptyset}}=\frac{V^{n}_{\lambda^{rc}\mu\nu}}{V^{n}_{\emptyset\emptyset\emptyset}}\cdot\frac{V^{n}_{\lambda\mu^{rc}\nu}}{V^{n}_{\emptyset\emptyset\emptyset}}\cdot\left(\frac{V^{n}_{\lambda^{rc}\mu^{rc}\nu}}{V^{n}_{\emptyset\emptyset\emptyset}}\right)^{-1}+q^{K_{1}(\lambda,\mu,\nu)}\cdot\frac{V^{n}_{\lambda^{r}\mu^{c}\nu}}{V^{n}_{\emptyset\emptyset\emptyset}}\cdot\frac{V^{n}_{\lambda^{c}\mu^{r}\nu}}{V^{n}_{\emptyset\emptyset\emptyset}}\cdot\left(\frac{V^{n}_{\lambda^{rc}\mu^{rc}\nu}}{V^{n}_{\emptyset\emptyset\emptyset}}\right)^{-1}

which generalizes the one in [
14
, Section 3.3] by Remark
4.18
.

However, this recurrence is still not enough to determine orbifold DT/PT vertex correspondence for the full 3-leg case unless one has 2-leg correspondence as the base case via Remark
4.8
. As explained in Introduction, due to the fewer symmetries of
V^{n}_{\lambda\mu\nu}
(resp.
W^{n}_{\lambda\mu\nu}
) than
V_{\lambda\mu\nu}
(resp.
W_{\lambda\mu\nu}
), one need to explore more graphical condensation recurrences for our orbifold case.

To derive the next graphical condensation recurrence, we take
\mathbf{G}
to be
\mathbf{H}(N;\lambda^{rc},\mu,\nu^{rc})
and

\displaystyle a:=\max S_{1}^{-},\;\;\;b:=\min S_{1}^{+},\;\;\;c:=\max S_{3}^{-},\;\;\;d:=\min S_{3}^{+}.

Then as above we have

\displaystyle\mathbf{G}-\{a,b,c,d\}=\mathbf{H}(N;\lambda,\mu,\nu),

\displaystyle\mathbf{G}-\{a,b\}=\mathbf{H}(N;\lambda,\mu,\nu^{rc}),

\displaystyle\mathbf{G}-\{c,d\}=\mathbf{H}(N;\lambda^{rc},\mu,\nu).

Similarly, the graph
\mathbf{G}-\{a,d\}
is obtained from the charge
-1
Maya diagram of
\lambda^{r}
, the Maya diagram of
\mu
, and the charge 1 Maya diagram of
\nu^{c}
, while the graph
\mathbf{G}-\{b,c\}
is obtained from the charge 1 Maya diagram of
\lambda^{c}
, the Maya diagram of
\mu
, and the charge
-1
Maya diagram of
\nu^{r}
. However, slightly different from the first case, 3D partitions asymptotic to
(\lambda^{r},\mu,\nu^{c})
correspond to dimer configurations on
\mathbf{G}-\{a,d\}
but with the orgin in
\mathbb{Z}^{3}
shifted directly to the face on the left-up side of the central face of
\mathbf{H}(N)
, and 3D partitions asymptotic to
(\lambda^{c},\mu,\nu^{r})
correspond to dimer configurations on
\mathbf{G}-\{b,c\}
but with the orgin in
\mathbb{Z}^{3}
shifted directly to the face on the right-down side of the central face of
\mathbf{H}(N)
. We omit the figures and one may take an exmple to draw (or modify) the pictures as in [
14
, Figure 5] to witness the required shift.

Let
\mathbf{D}_{\min}^{\nwarrow}(\lambda^{r},\mu,\nu^{c};N)
and
\mathbf{D}_{\min}^{\searrow}(\lambda^{c},\mu,\nu^{r};N)
be the minimal dimer configurations on
\mathbf{G}-\{a,d\}
and
\mathbf{G}-\{b,c\}
with their weights denoted by
q^{\omega_{\min}^{LU}(\lambda^{r},\mu,\nu^{c};N)}
and
q^{\omega_{\min}^{RD}(\lambda^{c},\mu,\nu^{r};N)}
respectively.
Let

\displaystyle\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}^{D}(\mathbf{H}(N;\lambda^{rc},\mu,\nu^{rc})-\{a,d\}):=\left(q^{\widetilde{\omega}_{\min}^{LU}(\lambda^{r},\mu,\nu^{c};N)}\right)^{-1}\cdot\mathbf{Z}^{D}(\mathbf{H}(N;\lambda^{rc},\mu,\nu^{rc})-\{a,d\}),

\displaystyle\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}^{D}(\mathbf{H}(N;\lambda^{rc},\mu,\nu^{rc})-\{b,c\}):=\left(q^{\widetilde{\omega}_{\min}^{RD}(\lambda^{c},\mu,\nu^{r};N)}\right)^{-1}\cdot\mathbf{Z}^{D}(\mathbf{H}(N;\lambda^{rc},\mu,\nu^{rc})-\{b,c\}),

where

\displaystyle q^{\widetilde{\omega}_{\min}^{LU}(\lambda^{r},\mu,\nu^{c};N)}:=q^{\omega_{\min}^{LU}(\lambda^{r},\mu,\nu^{c};N)}\cdot\prod\limits_{l=0}^{n-1}q_{l}^{|\mathrm{II}(\lambda^{r},\mu,\nu^{c})|_{l}}\cdot q_{l}^{2|\mathrm{III}(\lambda^{r},\mu,\nu^{c})|_{l}},

\displaystyle q^{\widetilde{\omega}_{\min}^{RD}}(\lambda^{c},\mu,\nu^{r};N):=q^{\omega_{\min}^{RD}(\lambda^{c},\mu,\nu^{r};N)}\cdot\prod\limits_{l=0}^{n-1}q_{l}^{|\mathrm{II}(\lambda^{c},\mu,\nu^{r})|_{l}}\cdot q_{l}^{2|\mathrm{III}(\lambda^{c},\mu,\nu^{r})|_{l}}.

As in the first case, we have

\displaystyle\lim_{N\to\infty}\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}^{D}(\mathbf{H}(N;\lambda^{rc},\mu,\nu^{rc})-\{a,d\})=V^{n}_{\lambda^{r}\mu\nu^{c}},

\displaystyle\lim_{N\to\infty}\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}^{D}(\mathbf{H}(N;\lambda^{rc},\mu,\nu^{rc})-\{b,c\})=V^{n}_{\lambda^{c}\mu\nu^{r}}.

and

\displaystyle\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}^{D}(\mathbf{H}(N;\lambda,\mu,\nu))\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}^{D}(\mathbf{H}(N;\lambda^{rc},\mu,\nu^{rc}))

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{q^{\widetilde{\omega}_{\min}(\lambda^{rc},\mu,\nu;N)}\cdot q^{\widetilde{\omega}_{\min}(\lambda,\mu,\nu^{rc};N)}}{q^{\widetilde{\omega}_{\min}(\lambda,\mu,\nu;N)}\cdot q^{\widetilde{\omega}_{\min}(\lambda^{rc},\mu,\nu^{rc};N)}}\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}^{D}(\mathbf{H}(N;\lambda^{rc},\mu,\nu))\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}^{D}(\mathbf{H}(N;\lambda,\mu,\nu^{rc}))

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\frac{q^{\widetilde{\omega}_{\min}^{LU}(\lambda^{r},\mu,\nu^{c};N)}\cdot q^{\widetilde{\omega}_{\min}^{RD}(\lambda^{c},\mu,\nu^{r};N)}}{q^{\widetilde{\omega}_{\min}(\lambda,\mu,\nu;N)}\cdot q^{\widetilde{\omega}_{\min}(\lambda^{rc},\mu,\nu^{rc};N)}}\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}^{D}(\mathbf{H}(N;\lambda^{rc},\mu,\nu^{rc})-\{a,d\})\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}^{D}(\mathbf{H}(N;\lambda^{rc},\mu,\nu^{rc})-\{b,c\})

It is shown in Section 4.3.2 that

\displaystyle\frac{q^{\widetilde{\omega}_{\min}(\lambda^{rc},\mu,\nu;N)}\cdot q^{\widetilde{\omega}_{\min}(\lambda,\mu,\nu^{rc};N)}}{q^{\widetilde{\omega}_{\min}(\lambda,\mu,\nu;N)}\cdot q^{\widetilde{\omega}_{\min}(\lambda^{rc},\mu,\nu^{rc};N)}}=1\;\;\;\;\;\;\mbox{and}\;\;\;\;\;\;\frac{q^{\widetilde{\omega}_{\min}^{LU}(\lambda^{r},\mu,\nu^{c};N)}\cdot q^{\widetilde{\omega}_{\min}^{RD}(\lambda^{c},\mu,\nu^{r};N)}}{q^{\widetilde{\omega}_{\min}(\lambda,\mu,\nu;N)}\cdot q^{\widetilde{\omega}_{\min}(\lambda^{rc},\mu,\nu^{rc};N)}}=q^{K_{2}(\lambda,\mu,\nu)}

where

q^{K_{2}(\lambda,\mu,\nu)}
defined in Lemma
4.26

is independent of
N
. Now let
N\to\infty
, we have

(8)

\displaystyle\frac{V^{n}_{\lambda\mu\nu}}{V^{n}_{\emptyset\emptyset\emptyset}}=\frac{V^{n}_{\lambda^{rc}\mu\nu}}{V^{n}_{\emptyset\emptyset\emptyset}}\cdot\frac{V^{n}_{\lambda\mu\nu^{rc}}}{V^{n}_{\emptyset\emptyset\emptyset}}\cdot\left(\frac{V^{n}_{\lambda^{rc}\mu\nu^{rc}}}{V^{n}_{\emptyset\emptyset\emptyset}}\right)^{-1}+q^{K_{2}(\lambda,\mu,\nu)}\cdot\frac{V^{n}_{\lambda^{r}\mu\nu^{c}}}{V^{n}_{\emptyset\emptyset\emptyset}}\cdot\frac{V^{n}_{\lambda^{c}\mu\nu^{r}}}{V^{n}_{\emptyset\emptyset\emptyset}}\cdot\left(\frac{V^{n}_{\lambda^{rc}\mu\nu^{rc}}}{V^{n}_{\emptyset\emptyset\emptyset}}\right)^{-1}.

As explained in Introduction, it is enough to determine the orbifold DT/PT vertex correspondence from the above two equations and [
45
, Theorem 5.22] together with the symmetries of
V^{n}_{\lambda\mu\nu}
and
W^{n}_{\lambda\mu\nu}
, see Section 5.3 for the orbifold PT case.
Since
d(\nu)=d(\nu^{\prime})
and
\widetilde{d}(\nu^{\prime})=\nu_{d(\nu)}
shown in Section 4.3, one can verify that (refer to Lemma
4.26
and Lemma
4.33
)

\displaystyle q^{K_{2}(\lambda,\mu,\nu)}=\overline{q^{K_{3}(\mu^{\prime},\lambda^{\prime},\nu^{\prime})}}.

In addition, by Remark
3.5
and Lemma
4.12
, we have

\displaystyle\frac{\overline{V}^{n}_{\mu^{\prime}\lambda^{\prime}\nu^{\prime}}}{\overline{V}^{n}_{\emptyset\emptyset\emptyset}}\cdot\frac{\overline{V}^{n}_{\mu^{\prime}(\lambda^{\prime})^{rc}(\nu^{\prime})^{rc}}}{\overline{V}^{n}_{\emptyset\emptyset\emptyset}}=\frac{\overline{V}^{n}_{\mu^{\prime}(\lambda^{\prime})^{rc}\nu^{\prime}}}{\overline{V}^{n}_{\emptyset\emptyset\emptyset}}\cdot\frac{\overline{V}^{n}_{\mu^{\prime}\lambda^{\prime}(\nu^{\prime})^{rc}}}{\overline{V}^{n}_{\emptyset\emptyset\emptyset}}+\overline{q^{K_{3}(\mu^{\prime},\lambda^{\prime},\nu^{\prime})}}\cdot\frac{\overline{V}^{n}_{\mu^{\prime}(\lambda^{\prime})^{c}(\nu^{\prime})^{r}}}{\overline{V}^{n}_{\emptyset\emptyset\emptyset}}\cdot\frac{\overline{V}^{n}_{\mu^{\prime}(\lambda^{\prime})^{r}(\nu^{\prime})^{c}}}{\overline{V}^{n}_{\emptyset\emptyset\emptyset}}

which is equivalent to Equation (
9
). Therefore, Equation (
9
) follows from Equation (
8
) and the symmetry of
V^{n}_{\lambda\mu\nu}
. Conversely, Equation (
8
) can again follow from Equation (
9
) and the symmetry of
V^{n}_{\lambda\mu\nu}
. However, Equation (
7
) doesn’t follow from Equation (
8
) or (
9
) due to the fewer symmetries of
V^{n}_{\lambda\mu\nu}
than
V_{\lambda\mu\nu}
in Remark
3.5
unless
n=1
.
For the completeness, we independently obtain the third graphical condensation recurrence (
9
) as follows.
Let

\displaystyle\mathbf{G}=\mathbf{H}(N;\lambda,\mu^{rc},\nu^{rc}),

\displaystyle\mathbf{G}-\{a,b,c,d\}=\mathbf{H}(N;\lambda,\mu,\nu),

\displaystyle\mathbf{G}-\{a,b\}=\mathbf{H}(N;\lambda,\mu,\nu^{rc}),

\displaystyle\mathbf{G}-\{c,d\}=\mathbf{H}(N;\lambda,\mu^{rc},\nu)

where

\displaystyle a:=\max S_{2}^{-},\;\;\;b:=\min S_{2}^{+},\;\;\;c:=\max S_{3}^{-},\;\;\;d:=\min S_{3}^{+}.

Now the graph
\mathbf{G}-\{a,d\}
(resp.
\mathbf{G}-\{b,c\}
) is obtained from the Maya diagram of
\lambda
, the charge
-1
(resp.
1
) Maya diagram of
\mu^{r}
(resp.
\mu^{c}
), and the charge 1 (resp.
-1
) Maya diagram of
\nu^{c}
(resp.
\nu^{r}
). And 3D partitions asymptotic to
(\lambda,\mu^{r},\nu^{c})
(resp.
(\lambda,\mu^{c},\nu^{r})
) correspond to dimer configurations on
\mathbf{G}-\{a,d\}
(resp.
\mathbf{G}-\{b,c\}
) but with the orgin in
\mathbb{Z}^{3}
shifted directly to the face on the left-down (resp. right-up) side of the central face of
\mathbf{H}(N)
. Let
\mathbf{D}_{\min}^{\swarrow}(\lambda^{r},\mu,\nu^{c};N)
and
\mathbf{D}_{\min}^{\nearrow}(\lambda^{c},\mu,\nu^{r};N)
be the minimal dimer configurations on
\mathbf{G}-\{a,d\}
and
\mathbf{G}-\{b,c\}
with their weights denoted by
q^{\omega_{\min}^{LD}(\lambda,\mu^{r},\nu^{c};N)}
and
q^{\omega_{\min}^{RU}(\lambda,\mu^{c},\nu^{r};N)}
respectively.
Let

\displaystyle\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}^{D}(\mathbf{H}(N;\lambda,\mu^{rc},\nu^{rc})-\{a,d\}):=\left(q^{\widetilde{\omega}_{\min}^{LD}(\lambda,\mu^{r},\nu^{c};N)}\right)^{-1}\cdot\mathbf{Z}^{D}(\mathbf{H}(N;\lambda,\mu^{rc},\nu^{rc})-\{a,d\}),

\displaystyle\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}^{D}(\mathbf{H}(N;\lambda,\mu^{rc},\nu^{rc})-\{b,c\}):=\left(q^{\widetilde{\omega}_{\min}^{RU}(\lambda,\mu^{c},\nu^{r};N)}\right)^{-1}\cdot\mathbf{Z}^{D}(\mathbf{H}(N;\lambda,\mu^{rc},\nu^{rc})-\{b,c\}),

where

\displaystyle q^{\widetilde{\omega}_{\min}^{LD}(\lambda,\mu^{r},\nu^{c};N)}:=q^{\omega_{\min}^{LD}(\lambda,\mu^{r},\nu^{c};N)}\cdot\prod\limits_{l=0}^{n-1}q_{l}^{|\mathrm{II}(\lambda,\mu^{r},\nu^{c})|_{l}}\cdot q_{l}^{2|\mathrm{III}(\lambda,\mu^{r},\nu^{c})|_{l}},

\displaystyle q^{\widetilde{\omega}_{\min}^{RU}}(\lambda,\mu^{c},\nu^{r};N):=q^{\omega_{\min}^{RU}(\lambda,\mu^{c},\nu^{r};N)}\cdot\prod\limits_{l=0}^{n-1}q_{l}^{|\mathrm{II}(\lambda,\mu^{c},\nu^{r})|_{l}}\cdot q_{l}^{2|\mathrm{III}(\lambda,\mu^{c},\nu^{r})|_{l}}.

Then we have

\displaystyle\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}^{D}(\mathbf{H}(N;\lambda,\mu,\nu))\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}^{D}(\mathbf{H}(N;\lambda,\mu^{rc},\nu^{rc}))

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{q^{\widetilde{\omega}_{\min}(\lambda,\mu^{rc},\nu;N)}\cdot q^{\widetilde{\omega}_{\min}(\lambda,\mu,\nu^{rc};N)}}{q^{\widetilde{\omega}_{\min}(\lambda,\mu,\nu;N)}\cdot q^{\widetilde{\omega}_{\min}(\lambda,\mu^{rc},\nu^{rc};N)}}\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}^{D}(\mathbf{H}(N;\lambda,\mu^{rc},\nu))\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}^{D}(\mathbf{H}(N;\lambda,\mu,\nu^{rc}))

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\frac{q^{\widetilde{\omega}_{\min}^{LD}(\lambda,\mu^{r},\nu^{c};N)}\cdot q^{\widetilde{\omega}_{\min}^{RU}(\lambda,\mu^{c},\nu^{r};N)}}{q^{\widetilde{\omega}_{\min}(\lambda,\mu,\nu;N)}\cdot q^{\widetilde{\omega}_{\min}(\lambda,\mu^{rc},\nu^{rc};N)}}\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}^{D}(\mathbf{H}(N;\lambda,\mu^{rc},\nu^{rc})-\{a,d\})\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}^{D}(\mathbf{H}(N;\lambda,\mu^{rc},\nu^{rc})-\{b,c\}).

It is computed in Section 4.3.3 that

\displaystyle\frac{q^{\widetilde{\omega}_{\min}(\lambda,\mu^{rc},\nu;N)}\cdot q^{\widetilde{\omega}_{\min}(\lambda,\mu,\nu^{rc};N)}}{q^{\widetilde{\omega}_{\min}(\lambda,\mu,\nu;N)}\cdot q^{\widetilde{\omega}_{\min}(\lambda,\mu^{rc},\nu^{rc};N)}}=1\;\;\;\;\;\;\mbox{and}\;\;\;\;\;\;\frac{q^{\widetilde{\omega}_{\min}^{LD}(\lambda,\mu^{r},\nu^{c};N)}\cdot q^{\widetilde{\omega}_{\min}^{RU}(\lambda,\mu^{c},\nu^{r};N)}}{q^{\widetilde{\omega}_{\min}(\lambda,\mu,\nu;N)}\cdot q^{\widetilde{\omega}_{\min}(\lambda,\mu^{rc},\nu^{rc};N)}}=q^{K_{3}(\lambda,\mu,\nu)}

where

q^{K_{3}(\lambda,\mu,\nu)}
defined in Lemma
4.33

is independent of
N
.
Since

\displaystyle\lim_{N\to\infty}\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}^{D}(\mathbf{H}(N;\lambda,\mu^{rc},\nu^{rc})-\{a,d\})=V^{n}_{\lambda\mu^{r}\nu^{c}},

\displaystyle\lim_{N\to\infty}\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}^{D}(\mathbf{H}(N;\lambda,\mu^{rc},\nu^{rc})-\{b,c\})=V^{n}_{\lambda\mu^{c}\nu^{r}},

then we have

(9)

\displaystyle\frac{V^{n}_{\lambda\mu\nu}}{V^{n}_{\emptyset\emptyset\emptyset}}=\frac{V^{n}_{\lambda\mu^{rc}\nu}}{V^{n}_{\emptyset\emptyset\emptyset}}\cdot\frac{V^{n}_{\lambda\mu\nu^{rc}}}{V^{n}_{\emptyset\emptyset\emptyset}}\cdot\left(\frac{V^{n}_{\lambda\mu^{rc}\nu^{rc}}}{V^{n}_{\emptyset\emptyset\emptyset}}\right)^{-1}+q^{K_{3}(\lambda,\mu,\nu)}\cdot\frac{V^{n}_{\lambda\mu^{r}\nu^{c}}}{V^{n}_{\emptyset\emptyset\emptyset}}\cdot\frac{V^{n}_{\lambda\mu^{c}\nu^{r}}}{V^{n}_{\emptyset\emptyset\emptyset}}\cdot\left(\frac{V^{n}_{\lambda\mu^{rc}\nu^{rc}}}{V^{n}_{\emptyset\emptyset\emptyset}}\right)^{-1}.

4.3.
Weights for orbifold DT theory

In order to formulate and compute weights for both orbifold DT and PT theories, we will first introduce some properties of partitions and their modified partitions in [
14
, Section 5.1] as follows.
For any partition
\eta\neq\emptyset
, let

\displaystyle d(\eta)=\max\{i:\eta_{i}\geq i\},\;\;\;\;\widetilde{d}(\eta)=\max\{i:\eta_{i}\geq d(\eta)\}.

Then we have
\ell(\eta)\geq\widetilde{d}(\eta)\geq d(\eta)\geq 1
. It is easy to verify that
d(\eta)=d(\eta^{\prime})
and
\widetilde{d}(\eta^{\prime})=\eta_{d(\eta)}
.

Lemma 4.7
.

([
14
, Lemma 5.1.9, 5.1.17 and Remark 5.1.31])

Let
\eta\neq\emptyset
be a partition. Then

\displaystyle\eta_{i}^{r}=\left\{\begin{aligned} &\eta_{i}+1,\;\;\;\mbox{if $i<d(\eta)$},\\
&\eta_{i+1},\;\;\;\;\;\;\mbox{if $i\geq d(\eta)$},\end{aligned}\right.

\displaystyle\eta_{i}^{c}=\left\{\begin{aligned} &\eta_{i}-1,\;\;\;\;\;\;\;\mbox{if $i\leq\widetilde{d}(\eta)$},\\
&d(\eta)-1,\;\;\;\mbox{if $i=\widetilde{d}(\eta)+1$},\\
&\eta_{i-1},\;\;\;\;\;\;\;\;\;\mbox{if $i>\widetilde{d}(\eta)+1$},\end{aligned}\right.

\displaystyle\eta_{i}^{rc}=\left\{\begin{aligned} &\eta_{i},\;\;\;\;\;\;\;\;\;\;\;\;\;\mbox{if $i<d(\eta)$},\\
&d(\eta)-1,\;\;\;\mbox{if $d(\eta)\leq i\leq\widetilde{d}(\eta)$},\\
&\eta_{i},\;\;\;\;\;\;\;\;\;\;\;\;\;\mbox{if $i>\widetilde{d}(\eta)$}.\end{aligned}\right.

Remark 4.8
.

It follows easily from [
14
, Remark 5.1.11, 5.1.27 and 5.1.28] that for any partition
\eta\neq\emptyset
, we have
|\eta^{r}|,|\eta^{c}|,|\eta^{rc}|<|\eta|
.

Lemma 4.9
.

([
14
, Remark 5.1.12, 5.1.19 and Lemma 5.1.34])
Let
\eta\neq\emptyset
be a partition. Then

(i)
if
d(\eta)=1
and
\eta_{1}=1
, then
\ell(\eta^{r})=\ell(\eta)-1
,
\ell(\eta^{c})=\ell(\eta^{rc})=0
;

(ii)
if
d(\eta)=1
and
\eta_{1}>1
, then
\ell(\eta^{r})=\ell(\eta)-1
,
\ell(\eta^{c})=1
and
\ell(\eta^{rc})=0
;

(iii)
if
d(\eta)>1
, then
\ell(\eta^{r})=\ell(\eta)-1
,
\ell(\eta^{c})=\ell(\eta)+1
and
\ell(\eta^{rc})=\ell(\eta)
.

Lemma 4.10
.

([
14
, Lemma 5.1.13 and Remark 5.1.22, 5.1.32])
Let
\eta\neq\emptyset
be a partition. Then

(i)

d(\eta^{r})=d(\eta)\Longleftrightarrow\eta_{d(\eta)+1}=d(\eta)
,
d(\eta^{r})=d(\eta)-1\Longleftrightarrow\eta_{d(\eta)+1}<d(\eta)
,

(ii)

d(\eta^{c})=d(\eta)\Longleftrightarrow\eta_{d(\eta)}>d(\eta)
,
d(\eta^{c})=d(\eta)-1\Longleftrightarrow\eta_{d(\eta)}=d(\eta)
,

(iii)

d(\eta^{rc})=d(\eta)-1
.

Remark 4.11
.

By Lemma
4.9
and Lemma
4.10
, we have

(i)
if
d(\eta)>1
and
d(\eta^{r})=d(\eta)-1
, then
\widetilde{d}(\eta)=d(\eta)
;

(ii)
if
d(\eta)>1
and
d(\eta^{r})=d(\eta)
, then
\widetilde{d}(\eta)\geq d(\eta)+1
and
\eta_{i}=d(\eta)
for
d(\eta)+1\leq i\leq\widetilde{d}(\eta)
.

(iii)
if
d(\eta)=1
and
\eta_{1}>1
or
\eta_{1}=1
, then
\widetilde{d}(\eta)=\ell(\eta)
and
\eta_{i}=1
for
2\leq i\leq\ell(\eta)
.

(iv)

\eta^{c}=\emptyset
if and only if
d(\eta)=1
and
\eta_{1}=1
, i.e.,
\eta_{i}=1
for
1\leq i\leq\ell(\eta)
.

Lemma 4.12
.

([
14
, Lemma 5.1.24 and Remark 5.1.30])
Let
\eta\neq\emptyset
be a partition. Then

\displaystyle(\eta^{c})^{\prime}=(\eta^{\prime})^{r};\;\;\;\;\;\;(\eta^{r})^{\prime}=(\eta^{\prime})^{c};\;\;\;\;\;\;(\eta^{rc})^{\prime}=(\eta^{\prime})^{rc}.

Lemma 4.13
.

([
14
, Lemma 5.1.16 and Lemma 5.1.23])
For any partition
\eta\neq\emptyset
, we have

(i)

\{i\in\mathbb{Z}_{>0}:\eta^{r}_{i}>i+1\}=\{i\in\mathbb{Z}_{>0}:i\leq d(\eta)-1\}
,

(ii)

\{i\in\mathbb{Z}_{>0}:\eta^{c}_{i}\geq i-1\}=\{i\in\mathbb{Z}_{>0}:i\leq d(\eta)\}
.

Next, we employ the following notations for simplifications later.

\displaystyle q^{\varpi_{1}(m,l,k)}:=\prod_{i=0}^{m}\prod_{j=0}^{m-l}q_{j-i+k}^{m-i},

\displaystyle q^{\varpi_{2}(\eta,m,k)}:=\prod_{i=1}^{\ell(\eta)}\prod_{j=0}^{m}q_{j-i+k}^{\eta_{i}},

\displaystyle q^{\varpi_{3}(\eta,m,k)}:=\prod_{i=1}^{\ell(\eta)}\prod_{j=0}^{\eta_{i}-1}q_{j-i+k}^{-m+i},

\displaystyle q^{\varpi_{4}(\eta,m,k)}:=\prod_{i:1\leq i\leq\eta_{i}}\prod_{j=1}^{i-k}q^{m+\eta_{i}-i+k-1}_{m-i+j+k-1}\cdot\prod_{i:\eta_{i}<i\leq\ell(\eta)}\prod_{j=1}^{\eta_{i}}q_{m-i+j+k-1}^{m+\eta_{i}-i+k-1},

\displaystyle q^{\varpi_{5}(\eta,m,k)}:=\prod_{i:1\leq i\leq\eta_{i}}\prod_{j=1}^{i-k}q_{i-j-k-m+1}^{\eta_{i}-j}\cdot\prod_{i:\eta_{i}<i\leq\ell(\eta)}\prod_{j=1}^{\eta_{i}}q_{i-j-k-m+1}^{\eta_{i}-j},

\displaystyle q^{\varpi_{6}(\eta,m)}:=\left(q_{m}^{m+\eta_{1}}\right)^{-1}\cdot\prod_{i:2\leq i\leq\eta_{i}+1}\prod_{j=1}^{i-2}q_{m+j+1-i}^{m+1+\eta_{i}-i}\cdot\prod_{i:\eta_{i}+1<i\leq\ell(\eta)}\prod_{j=1}^{\eta_{i}}q_{m+j+1-i}^{m+1+\eta_{i}-i},

\displaystyle q^{\varpi_{7}(\eta,m)}:=\left(q^{\eta_{1}}_{-m}\right)^{-1}\cdot\prod_{i:2\leq i\leq\eta_{i}+1}\prod_{j=1}^{i-2}q_{i-j-m-1}^{\eta_{i}-j}\cdot\prod_{i:\eta_{i}+1<i\leq\ell(\eta)}\prod_{j=1}^{\eta_{i}}q_{i-j-m-1}^{\eta_{i}-j},

where
m,l,k
are all integers and
\eta
is a partition. And for any function
F(\{q_{k}\})
of variables
\{q_{k}\}
, the notation
\overline{F(\{q_{k}\})}
denotes
F(\{q_{k}\})\big|_{q_{k}\leftrightarrow q_{-k}}
throughout this paper.

4.3.1.
Weights for the graphical condensation recurrence with
\mathbf{G}=\mathbf{H}(N;\lambda^{rc},\mu^{rc},\nu)

In this subsection, we will compute the weights of several dimer configurations as in [
14
, Section 5.2] with the given weighted rule in Definition
4.3
. The minimal dimer configuration of
\mathbf{H}(N)
corresponding to the empty 3D partition has the weight from the
N^{2}
horizontal dimers (see the tilings on the floor of the rightmost picture in [
14
, Figure 15] for
\mathbf{H}(5)
as an example), which is

\displaystyle\prod_{i=0}^{N-1}\prod_{j=0}^{N-1}q_{j-i}^{N-1-i}.

The dimer configuration corresponding to the 3D partition
\pi_{\min}(\lambda,\mu,\nu)
with
N(|\lambda|+|\mu|+|\nu|)-|\mathrm{II}(\lambda,\mu,\nu)|-2|\mathrm{III}(\lambda,\mu,\nu)|
boxes has the weight computed as follows

\displaystyle\dfrac{\prod\limits_{i=0}^{N-1}\prod\limits_{j=0}^{N-1}q_{j-i}^{N-1-i}\cdot\prod\limits_{i=1}^{\ell(\lambda^{\prime})}\prod\limits_{j=0}^{N-1}q_{j-i+1}^{\lambda^{\prime}_{i}}\cdot\prod\limits_{i=1}^{\ell(\mu)}\prod\limits_{j=0}^{N-1}q_{i-j-1}^{\mu_{i}}\cdot\prod\limits_{i=1}^{\ell(\nu)}\prod\limits_{j=0}^{\nu_{i}-1}q_{j-i+1}^{N}}{\prod\limits_{(i,j,k)\in\mathrm{II}(\lambda,\mu,\nu)}q_{i-j}\cdot\prod\limits_{(i,j,k)\in\mathrm{III}(\lambda,\mu,\nu)}q_{i-j}^{2}}.

The minimal dimer configuration of
\mathbf{H}(N;\lambda,\mu,\nu)
has more horizontal dimers in sector 1 and 2 and fewer horizontal dimers in sector 3 than the above dimer configuration. In fact, in sector 1, if
(\lambda^{\prime})_{i}\geq i
, the
i
-the part of
\lambda^{\prime}

contributes
(i-1)
more horizontal dimers with weights:
q_{N+1-i}^{N-i+\lambda^{\prime}_{i}},\cdots,q_{N-1}^{N-i+\lambda^{\prime}_{i}}
; if
\lambda^{\prime}_{i}<i
, the
i
-th part of
\lambda^{\prime}
contributes
\lambda^{\prime}_{i}
more horizontal dimers with weights:
q_{N+1-i}^{N-i+\lambda^{\prime}_{i}},\cdots,q_{N-i+\lambda^{\prime}_{i}}^{N-i+\lambda^{\prime}_{i}}
.
In sector 2, if
\mu_{i}\geq i
, the
i
-th part of
\mu
contributes
(i-1)
more horizontal dimers with weights:
q_{i-1-N}^{\mu_{i}-1},q_{i-2-N}^{\mu_{i}-2},\cdots,q_{1-N}^{\mu_{i}-i+1}
; if
\mu_{i}<i
, the
i
-th part of
\mu
contributes
\mu_{i}
more horizontal dimers with weights:
q_{i-1-N}^{\mu_{i}-1},q_{i-2-N}^{\mu_{i}-2},\cdots,q_{i-\mu_{i}-N}^{0}
. In sector 3, the horizontal dimers which are not in the minimal dimer configuration of
\mathbf{H}(N;\lambda,\mu,\nu)
have the weight
\prod\limits_{i=1}^{\ell(\nu)}\prod\limits_{j=0}^{\nu_{i}-1}q_{j-i+1}^{2N-i}
.
Then we have

Lemma 4.14
.

The minimal dimer configuration of
\mathbf{H}(N;\lambda,\mu,\nu)
has the weight

q^{\omega_{\min}(\lambda,\mu,\nu;N)}=q^{\widetilde{\omega}_{\min}(\lambda,\mu,\nu;N)}\cdot\prod\limits_{l=0}^{n-1}q_{l}^{-|\mathrm{II}(\lambda,\mu,\nu)|_{l}}\cdot q_{l}^{-2|\mathrm{III}(\lambda,\mu,\nu)|_{l}}
, where

\displaystyle q^{\widetilde{\omega}_{\min}(\lambda,\mu,\nu;N)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\prod_{i=0}^{N-1}\prod_{j=0}^{N-1}q_{j-i}^{N-1-i}\cdot\prod_{i=1}^{\ell(\lambda^{\prime})}\prod_{j=0}^{N-1}q_{j-i+1}^{\lambda^{\prime}_{i}}\cdot\prod_{i=1}^{\ell(\mu)}\prod_{j=0}^{N-1}q_{i-j-1}^{\mu_{i}}\cdot\prod_{i=1}^{\ell(\nu)}\prod_{j=0}^{\nu_{i}-1}q_{j-i+1}^{N}\cdot\prod_{i=1}^{\ell(\nu)}\prod_{j=0}^{\nu_{i}-1}q_{j-i+1}^{-2N+i}

\displaystyle\times\prod_{i:1\leq i\leq\lambda^{\prime}_{i}}\prod_{j=1}^{i-1}q_{N-i+j}^{N-i+\lambda^{\prime}_{i}}\cdot\prod_{i:\lambda^{\prime}_{i}<i\leq\ell(\lambda^{\prime})}\prod_{j=1}^{\lambda^{\prime}_{i}}q_{N-i+j}^{N-i+\lambda^{\prime}_{i}}\cdot\prod_{i:1\leq i\leq\mu_{i}}\prod_{j=1}^{i-1}q_{i-j-N}^{\mu_{i}-j}\cdot\prod_{i:\mu_{i}<i\leq\ell(\mu)}\prod_{j=1}^{\mu_{i}}q_{i-j-N}^{\mu_{i}-j}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle q^{\varpi_{1}(N-1,0,0)}\cdot q^{\varpi_{2}(\lambda^{\prime},N-1,1)}\cdot\overline{q^{\varpi_{2}(\mu,N-1,1)}}\cdot q^{\varpi_{3}(\nu,N,1)}\cdot q^{\varpi_{4}(\lambda^{\prime},N,1)}\cdot q^{\varpi_{5}(\mu,N,1)}.

Similarly, we have the following

Lemma 4.15
.

The minimal dimer configuration of
\mathbf{H}(N;\lambda^{rc},\mu^{rc},\nu)-\{a,d\}
has the weight

q^{\omega_{\min}^{U}(\lambda^{r},\mu^{c},\nu;N)}=q^{\widetilde{\omega}_{\min}^{U}(\lambda^{r},\mu^{c},\nu;N)}\cdot\prod\limits_{l=0}^{n-1}q_{l}^{-|\mathrm{II}(\lambda^{r},\mu^{c},\nu)|_{l}}\cdot q_{l}^{-2|\mathrm{III}(\lambda^{r},\mu^{c},\nu)|_{l}}
, where

\displaystyle q^{\widetilde{\omega}^{U}_{\min}(\lambda^{r},\mu^{c},\nu;N)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\prod_{i=0}^{N}\prod_{j=0}^{N}q_{j-i}^{N-i}\cdot\prod_{i=1}^{\ell((\lambda^{r})^{\prime})}\prod_{j=0}^{N}q_{j-i+1}^{(\lambda^{r})^{\prime}_{i}}\cdot\prod_{i=1}^{\ell(\mu^{c})}\prod_{j=0}^{N}q_{i-j-1}^{\mu^{c}_{i}}\cdot\prod_{i=1}^{\ell(\nu)}\prod_{j=0}^{\nu_{i}-1}q_{j-i+1}^{N-1}\cdot\prod_{i=1}^{\ell(\nu)}\prod_{j=0}^{\nu_{i}-1}q_{j-i+1}^{-2N+i}

\displaystyle\times\left(q_{N}^{N+(\lambda^{r})^{\prime}_{1}}\right)^{-1}\cdot\prod_{i:2\leq i\leq(\lambda^{r})^{\prime}_{i}+1}\prod_{j=1}^{i-2}q_{N+j+1-i}^{N+1+(\lambda^{r})^{\prime}_{i}-i}\cdot\prod_{i:(\lambda^{r})^{\prime}_{i}+1<i\leq\ell((\lambda^{r})^{\prime})}\prod_{j=1}^{(\lambda^{r})^{\prime}_{i}}q_{N+j+1-i}^{N+1+(\lambda^{r})^{\prime}_{i}-i}

\displaystyle\times\left(q^{\mu^{c}_{1}}_{-N}\right)^{-1}\prod_{i:2\leq i\leq\mu^{c}_{i}+1}\prod_{j=1}^{i-2}q_{i-j-N-1}^{\mu^{c}_{i}-j}\cdot\prod_{i:\mu^{c}_{i}+1<i\leq\ell(\mu^{c})}\prod_{j=1}^{\mu^{c}_{i}}q_{i-j-N-1}^{\mu^{c}_{i}-j}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle q^{\varpi_{1}(N,0,0)}\cdot q^{\varpi_{2}((\lambda^{\prime})^{c},N,1)}\cdot\overline{q^{\varpi_{2}(\mu^{c},N,1)}}\cdot q^{\varpi_{3}(\nu,N+1,1)}\cdot q^{\varpi_{6}((\lambda^{\prime})^{c},N)}\cdot q^{\varpi_{7}(\mu^{c},N)}.

Lemma 4.16
.

The minimal dimer configuration of
\mathbf{H}(N;\lambda^{rc},\mu^{rc},\nu)-\{b,c\}
has the weight

q^{\omega_{\min}^{D}(\lambda^{c},\mu^{r},\nu;N)}=q^{\widetilde{\omega}_{\min}^{D}}(\lambda^{c},\mu^{r},\nu;N)\cdot\prod\limits_{l=0}^{n-1}q_{l}^{-|\mathrm{II}(\lambda^{c},\mu^{r},\nu)|_{l}}\cdot q_{l}^{-2|\mathrm{III}(\lambda^{c},\mu^{r},\nu)|_{l}}
, where

\displaystyle q^{\widetilde{\omega}^{D}_{\min}(\lambda^{c},\mu^{r},\nu;N)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\prod_{i=0}^{N-2}\prod_{j=0}^{N-2}q_{j-i}^{N-i-2}\cdot\prod_{i=1}^{\ell((\lambda^{c})^{\prime})}\prod_{j=0}^{N-2}q_{j-i+1}^{(\lambda^{c})^{\prime}_{i}}\cdot\prod_{i=1}^{\ell(\mu^{r})}\prod_{j=0}^{N-2}q_{i-j-1}^{\mu^{r}_{i}}\cdot\prod_{i=1}^{\ell(\nu)}\prod_{j=0}^{\nu_{i}-1}q_{j-i+1}^{N+1}\cdot\prod_{i=1}^{\ell(\nu)}\prod_{j=0}^{\nu_{i}-1}q_{j-i+1}^{-2N+i}

\displaystyle\times\prod_{i:1\leq i\leq(\lambda^{c})_{i}^{\prime}}\prod_{j=1}^{i}q^{N+(\lambda^{c})_{i}^{\prime}-i-1}_{N-i+j-1}\cdot\prod_{i:(\lambda^{c})_{i}^{\prime}<i\leq\ell((\lambda^{c})^{\prime})}\prod_{j=1}^{(\lambda^{c})_{i}^{\prime}}q_{N-i+j-1}^{N+(\lambda^{c})_{i}^{\prime}-i-1}

\displaystyle\times\prod_{i:1\leq i\leq\mu^{r}_{i}}\prod_{j=1}^{i}q_{i-j-N+1}^{\mu^{r}_{i}-j}\cdot\prod_{i:\mu^{r}_{i}<i\leq\ell(\mu^{r})}\prod_{j=1}^{\mu^{r}_{i}}q_{i-j-N+1}^{\mu^{r}_{i}-j}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle q^{\varpi_{1}(N-2,0,0)}\cdot q^{\varpi_{2}((\lambda^{\prime})^{r},N-2,1)}\cdot\overline{q^{\varpi_{2}(\mu^{r},N-2,1)}}\cdot q^{\varpi_{3}(\nu,N-1,1)}\cdot q^{\varpi_{4}((\lambda^{\prime})^{r},N,0)}\cdot q^{\varpi_{5}(\mu^{r},N,0)}.

Now it follows from Lemma
4.14
that

\displaystyle\frac{q^{\widetilde{\omega}_{\min}(\lambda^{rc},\mu,\nu;N)}\cdot q^{\widetilde{\omega}_{\min}(\lambda,\mu^{rc},\nu;N)}}{q^{\widetilde{\omega}_{\min}(\lambda,\mu,\nu;N)}\cdot q^{\widetilde{\omega}_{\min}(\lambda^{rc},\mu^{rc},\nu;N)}}=1.

We still need the following lemmas to obtain the graphical condensation recurrence (
7
).

Lemma 4.17
.

For any partitions
\lambda,\mu\neq\emptyset
and
\nu
, we have

\displaystyle\frac{q^{\widetilde{\omega}_{\min}^{U}(\lambda^{r},\mu^{c},\nu;N)}\cdot q^{\widetilde{\omega}_{\min}^{D}(\lambda^{c},\mu^{r},\nu;N)}}{q^{\widetilde{\omega}_{\min}(\lambda,\mu,\nu;N)}\cdot q^{\widetilde{\omega}_{\min}(\lambda^{rc},\mu^{rc},\nu;N)}}=q^{K_{1}(\lambda,\mu,\nu)}

where

\displaystyle q^{K_{1}(\lambda,\mu,\nu)}:=q_{-\widetilde{d}(\lambda^{\prime})}^{d(\lambda^{\prime})}\cdot q_{\widetilde{d}(\mu)}^{d(\mu)}\cdot\prod\limits_{i=-\widetilde{d}(\lambda^{\prime})}^{\widetilde{d}(\mu)}q_{i}^{-1}\cdot\prod\limits_{i=\widetilde{d}(\lambda^{\prime})+1}^{\ell(\lambda^{\prime})}\left(\frac{q_{-i}}{q_{-i+1}}\right)^{\lambda^{\prime}_{i}}\cdot\prod\limits_{i=\widetilde{d}(\mu)+1}^{\ell(\mu)}\left(\frac{q_{i}}{q_{i-1}}\right)^{\mu_{i}}

is independent of
N
.

Proof.

It follows from Lemmas
4.14
,
4.15
,
4.16
,
4.19
,
4.20
,
4.21
,
4.22
,
4.23
. ∎

Remark 4.18
.

When
n=1
, i.e., all
q_{l}
are equal to
q
, then we have

\displaystyle q^{K_{1}(\lambda,\mu,\nu)}:=q^{d(\lambda^{\prime})+d(\mu)-\widetilde{d}(\mu)-\widetilde{d}(\lambda^{\prime})-1}=q^{d(\lambda)+d(\mu)-\mu^{\prime}_{d(\mu)}-\lambda_{d(\lambda)}-1}

which is equal to
q^{-K}
in [
14
, Section 5.2].

Lemma 4.19
.

\displaystyle\frac{q^{\varpi_{1}(N,0,0)}\cdot q^{\varpi_{1}(N-2,0,0)}}{\left(q^{\varpi_{1}(N-1,0,0)}\right)^{2}}=q_{N}^{N}\cdot\prod_{i=1-N}^{N-1}q_{i}.

Lemma 4.20
.

For any partition
\eta\neq\emptyset
, we have

\displaystyle\frac{q^{\varpi_{2}(\eta^{c},N,1)}\cdot q^{\varpi_{2}(\eta^{r},N-2,1)}}{q^{\varpi_{2}(\eta,N-1,1)}\cdot q^{\varpi_{2}(\eta^{rc},N-1,1)}}=q_{-\widetilde{d}(\eta)}^{d(\eta)-1}\cdot\prod\limits_{i=\widetilde{d}(\eta)+1}^{\ell(\eta)}\left(\frac{q_{-i}}{q_{-i+1}}\right)^{\eta_{i}}\cdot\frac{\prod\limits_{i=1}^{\widetilde{d}(\eta)}q_{N-i+1}^{\eta_{i}-1}\cdot\prod\limits_{i=0}^{N-1}q_{i-\widetilde{d}(\eta)+1}^{-1}}{\prod\limits_{i=1}^{d(\eta)-1}q_{N-i}^{\eta_{i}+1}\cdot\prod\limits_{i=N-\widetilde{d}(\eta)+1}^{N-d(\eta)}q_{i}^{d(\eta)}}.

Proof.

If
d(\eta)>1
, by Lemma
4.7
, Lemma
4.9
and Remark
4.11
, we have

\displaystyle\frac{q^{\varpi_{2}(\eta^{c},N,1)}}{q^{\varpi_{2}(\eta,N-1,1)}}=\frac{\prod\limits_{i=1}^{\widetilde{d}(\eta)}\prod\limits_{j=0}^{N}q_{j-i+1}^{\eta_{i}-1}\cdot\prod\limits_{j=0}^{N}q_{j-\widetilde{d}(\eta)}^{d(\eta)-1}\cdot\prod\limits_{i=\widetilde{d}(\eta)+2}^{\ell(\eta)+1}\prod\limits_{j=0}^{N}q_{j-i+1}^{\eta_{i-1}}}{\prod\limits_{i=1}^{\widetilde{d}(\eta)}\prod\limits_{j=0}^{N-1}q_{j-i+1}^{\eta_{i}}\cdot\prod\limits_{i=\widetilde{d}(\eta)+1}^{\ell(\eta)}\prod\limits_{j=0}^{N-1}q_{j-i+1}^{\eta_{i}}}=\frac{\prod\limits_{i=1}^{\widetilde{d}(\eta)}q_{N-i+1}^{\eta_{i}-1}\cdot\prod\limits_{j=0}^{N}q_{j-\widetilde{d}(\eta)}^{d(\eta)-1}\cdot\prod\limits_{i=\widetilde{d}(\eta)+1}^{\ell(\eta)}q_{-i}^{\eta_{i}}}{\prod\limits_{i=1}^{\widetilde{d}(\eta)}\prod\limits_{j=0}^{N-1}q_{j-i+1}}

and

\displaystyle\frac{q^{\varpi_{2}(\eta^{r},N-2,1)}}{q^{\varpi_{2}(\eta^{rc},N-1,1)}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\prod\limits_{i=1}^{d(\eta)-1}\prod\limits_{j=0}^{N-2}q_{j-i+1}^{\eta_{i}+1}\cdot\prod\limits_{i=d(\eta)}^{\ell(\eta)-1}\prod\limits_{j=0}^{N-2}q_{j-i+1}^{\eta_{i+1}}}{\prod\limits_{i=1}^{d(\eta)-1}\prod\limits_{j=0}^{N-1}q_{j-i+1}^{\eta_{i}}\cdot\prod\limits_{i=d(\eta)}^{\widetilde{d}(\eta)}\prod\limits_{j=0}^{N-1}q_{j-i+1}^{d(\eta)-1}\cdot\prod\limits_{i=\widetilde{d}(\eta)+1}^{\ell(\eta)}\prod\limits_{j=0}^{N-1}q_{j-i+1}^{\eta_{i}}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\prod\limits_{i=1}^{d(\eta)-1}\prod\limits_{j=0}^{N-2}q_{j-i+1}\cdot\prod\limits_{i=d(\eta)+1}^{\widetilde{d}(\eta)}\prod\limits_{j=1}^{N-1}q_{j-i+1}}{\prod\limits_{i=1}^{d(\eta)-1}q_{N-i}^{\eta_{i}}\cdot\prod\limits_{j=0}^{N-1}q_{j-d(\eta)+1}^{d(\eta)-1}\cdot\prod\limits_{i=d(\eta)+1}^{\widetilde{d}(\eta)}q_{-i+1}^{d(\eta)-1}\cdot\prod\limits_{i=\widetilde{d}(\eta)+1}^{\ell(\eta)}q_{-i+1}^{\eta_{i}}}.

Then

\displaystyle\frac{q^{\varpi_{2}(\eta^{c},N,1)}\cdot q^{\varpi_{2}(\eta^{r},N-2,1)}}{q^{\varpi_{2}(\eta,N-1,1)}\cdot q^{\varpi_{2}(\eta^{rc},N-1,1)}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\prod\limits_{i=1}^{\widetilde{d}(\eta)}q_{N-i+1}^{\eta_{i}-1}\cdot\prod\limits_{j=0}^{N}q_{j-\widetilde{d}(\eta)}^{d(\eta)-1}\cdot\prod\limits_{i=\widetilde{d}(\eta)+1}^{\ell(\eta)}q_{-i}^{\eta_{i}}}{\prod\limits_{i=1}^{d(\eta)-1}q_{N-i}^{\eta_{i}+1}\cdot\prod\limits_{j=1-\widetilde{d}(\eta)}^{N-d(\eta)}q_{j}^{d(\eta)-1}\cdot\prod\limits_{i=\widetilde{d}(\eta)+1}^{\ell(\eta)}q_{-i+1}^{\eta_{i}}\cdot\prod\limits_{i=d(\eta)+1}^{\widetilde{d}(\eta)}q_{-i+1}\cdot\prod\limits_{j=0}^{N-1}q_{j-d(\eta)+1}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle q_{-\widetilde{d}(\eta)}^{d(\eta)-1}\cdot\prod\limits_{i=\widetilde{d}(\eta)+1}^{\ell(\eta)}\left(\frac{q_{-i}}{q_{-i+1}}\right)^{\eta_{i}}\cdot\frac{\prod\limits_{i=1}^{\widetilde{d}(\eta)}q_{N-i+1}^{\eta_{i}-1}\cdot\prod\limits_{j=0}^{N-1}q_{j-\widetilde{d}(\eta)+1}^{-1}}{\prod\limits_{i=1}^{d(\eta)-1}q_{N-i}^{\eta_{i}+1}\cdot\prod\limits_{j=N-\widetilde{d}(\eta)+1}^{N-d(\eta)}q_{j}^{d(\eta)}}.

If
d(\eta)=1
and
\eta_{1}>1
, then by Remark
4.11

\displaystyle\frac{q^{\varpi_{2}(\eta^{c},N,1)}\cdot q^{\varpi_{2}(\eta^{r},N-2,1)}}{q^{\varpi_{2}(\eta,N-1,1)}\cdot q^{\varpi_{2}(\eta^{rc},N-1,1)}}=\frac{\prod\limits_{j=0}^{N}q_{j}^{\eta_{1}-1}\prod\limits_{i=1}^{\ell(\eta)-1}\prod\limits_{j=0}^{N-2}q_{j-i+1}^{\eta_{i+1}}}{\prod\limits_{i=1}^{\ell(\eta)}\prod\limits_{j=0}^{N-1}q_{j-i+1}^{\eta_{i}}}=\frac{q_{N}^{\eta_{1}}}{\prod\limits_{j=1-\ell(\eta)}^{N}q_{j}}.

Similarly, if
d(\eta)=1
and
\eta_{1}=1
, we have

\displaystyle\frac{q^{\varpi_{2}(\eta^{c},N,1)}\cdot q^{\varpi_{2}(\eta^{r},N-2,1)}}{q^{\varpi_{2}(\eta,N-1,1)}\cdot q^{\varpi_{2}(\eta^{rc},N-1,1)}}=\frac{\prod\limits_{i=1}^{\ell(\eta)-1}\prod\limits_{j=0}^{N-2}q_{j-i+1}^{\eta_{i+1}}}{\prod\limits_{i=1}^{\ell(\eta)}\prod\limits_{j=0}^{N-1}q_{j-i+1}^{\eta_{i}}}=\frac{1}{\prod\limits_{j=1-\ell(\eta)}^{N-1}q_{j}}.

Now the proof is completed.
∎

Lemma 4.21
.

For any partition
\eta
, we have

\displaystyle\frac{q^{\varpi_{3}(\eta,N+1,1)}q^{\varpi_{3}(\eta,N-1,1)}}{\left(q^{\varpi_{3}(\eta,N,1)}\right)^{2}}=1.

Lemma 4.22
.

For any partition
\eta\neq\emptyset
, we have

\displaystyle\frac{q^{\varpi_{6}(\eta^{c},N)}\cdot q^{\varpi_{4}(\eta^{r},N,0)}}{q^{\varpi_{4}(\eta,N,1)}\cdot q^{\varpi_{4}(\eta^{rc},N,1)}}=\frac{\prod\limits_{i=1}^{d(\eta)-1}q^{\eta_{i}-i}_{N-i}}{q_{N}^{N}\cdot\prod\limits_{i=1}^{d(\eta)}q_{N-i+1}^{\eta_{i}-i}}.

Proof.

If
d(\eta)>1
and
d(\eta^{r})=d(\eta)
, then by Lemmas
4.7
,
4.9
,
4.10
,
4.13
and Remark
4.11

\displaystyle\frac{q^{\varpi_{6}(\eta^{c},N)}}{q^{\varpi_{4}(\eta,N,1)}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\prod\limits_{i=2}^{d(\eta)}\prod\limits_{j=2}^{i-1}q_{N+j-i}^{N+\eta_{i}-i}\cdot\prod\limits_{i=d(\eta)+1}^{\widetilde{d}(\eta)}\prod\limits_{j=2}^{\eta_{i}}q_{N+j-i}^{N+\eta_{i}-i}\cdot\prod\limits_{j=1}^{d(\eta)-1}q_{N+j-\widetilde{d}(\eta)}^{N+d(\eta)-\widetilde{d}(\eta)-1}\cdot\prod\limits_{i=\widetilde{d}(\eta)+1}^{\ell(\eta)}\prod\limits_{j=1}^{\eta_{i}}q_{N+j-i}^{N+\eta_{i}-i}}{q_{N}^{N+\eta_{1}-1}\cdot\prod\limits_{i=2}^{d(\eta)}\prod\limits_{j=1}^{i-1}q_{N-i+j}^{N+\eta_{i}-i}\cdot\prod\limits_{i=d(\eta)+1}^{\widetilde{d}(\eta)}\prod\limits_{j=1}^{\eta_{i}}q_{N-i+j}^{N+\eta_{i}-i}\cdot\prod\limits_{i=\widetilde{d}(\eta)+1}^{\ell(\eta)}\prod\limits_{j=1}^{\eta_{i}}q_{N-i+j}^{N+\eta_{i}-i}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\prod\limits_{j=1}^{d(\eta)-1}q_{N+j-\widetilde{d}(\eta)}^{N+d(\eta)-\widetilde{d}(\eta)-1}}{\prod\limits_{i=1}^{d(\eta)}q_{N-i+1}^{N+\eta_{i}-i}\cdot\prod\limits_{i=d(\eta)+1}^{\widetilde{d}(\eta)}q_{N-i+1}^{N+d(\eta)-i}}

and

\displaystyle\frac{q^{\varpi_{4}(\eta^{r},N,0)}}{q^{\varpi_{4}(\eta^{rc},N,1)}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\prod\limits_{i=1}^{d(\eta)-1}\prod\limits_{j=0}^{i-1}q^{N+\eta_{i}-i}_{N-i+j}\cdot\prod\limits_{j=1}^{d(\eta)}q^{N-1}_{N-d(\eta)+j-1}\cdot\prod\limits_{i=d(\eta)+2}^{\ell(\eta)}\prod\limits_{j=1}^{\eta_{i}}q_{N-i+j}^{N+\eta_{i}-i}}{\prod\limits_{i=1}^{d(\eta)-1}\prod\limits_{j=1}^{i-1}q_{N-i+j}^{N-i+\eta_{i}}\cdot\prod\limits_{i=d(\eta)}^{\widetilde{d}(\eta)}\prod\limits_{j=1}^{d(\eta)-1}q_{N-i+j}^{N-i+d(\eta)-1}\cdot\prod\limits_{i=\widetilde{d}(\eta)+1}^{\ell(\eta)}\prod\limits_{j=1}^{\eta_{i}}q_{N-i+j}^{N-i+\eta_{i}}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\prod\limits_{i=1}^{d(\eta)-1}q^{N+\eta_{i}-i}_{N-i}\cdot\prod\limits_{i=d(\eta)+1}^{\widetilde{d}(\eta)}q_{N+d(\eta)-i}^{N+d(\eta)-i}\cdot\prod\limits_{i=d(\eta)+1}^{\widetilde{d}(\eta)}\prod\limits_{j=1}^{d(\eta)-1}q_{N-i+j}}{\prod\limits_{j=1}^{d(\eta)-1}q_{N-d(\eta)+j}^{N-1}}.

Then

\displaystyle\frac{q^{\varpi_{6}(\eta^{c},N)}\cdot q^{\varpi_{4}(\eta^{r},N,0)}}{q^{\varpi_{4}(\eta,N,1)}\cdot q^{\varpi_{4}(\eta^{rc},N,1)}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\prod\limits_{j=1}^{d(\eta)-1}q_{N+j-\widetilde{d}(\eta)}^{N+d(\eta)-\widetilde{d}(\eta)-1}\cdot\prod\limits_{i=d(\eta)+1}^{\widetilde{d}(\eta)}q_{N+d(\eta)-i}^{N+d(\eta)-i}}{\prod\limits_{i=d(\eta)+1}^{\widetilde{d}(\eta)}q_{N-i+1}^{N+d(\eta)-i}\cdot\prod\limits_{j=1}^{d(\eta)-1}q_{N-d(\eta)+j}^{N-1}}\cdot\frac{\prod\limits_{i=1}^{d(\eta)-1}q^{N+\eta_{i}-i}_{N-i}\cdot\prod\limits_{i=d(\eta)+1}^{\widetilde{d}(\eta)}\prod\limits_{j=1}^{d(\eta)-1}q_{N-i+j}}{\prod\limits_{i=1}^{d(\eta)}q_{N-i+1}^{N+\eta_{i}-i}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\prod\limits_{j=1}^{d(\eta)-1}q_{N+j-\widetilde{d}(\eta)}^{d(\eta)-\widetilde{d}(\eta)-1}\cdot\prod\limits_{i=d(\eta)}^{\widetilde{d}(\eta)}\prod\limits_{j=1}^{d(\eta)-1}q_{N-i+j}\cdot\prod\limits_{i=d(\eta)+1}^{\widetilde{d}(\eta)}q_{N+d(\eta)-i}^{d(\eta)-i}}{\prod\limits_{i=d(\eta)+1}^{\widetilde{d}(\eta)}q_{N-i+1}^{d(\eta)-i}}\cdot\frac{\prod\limits_{i=1}^{d(\eta)-1}q^{\eta_{i}-i}_{N-i}}{q_{N}^{N}\cdot\prod\limits_{i=1}^{d(\eta)}q_{N-i+1}^{\eta_{i}-i}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\prod\limits_{i=1}^{d(\eta)-1}q^{\eta_{i}-i}_{N-i}}{q_{N}^{N}\cdot\prod\limits_{i=1}^{d(\eta)}q_{N-i+1}^{\eta_{i}-i}}.

The proof is completed by using the above similar argument for the other cases.
∎

Lemma 4.23
.

For any partition
\eta\neq\emptyset
, we have

\displaystyle\frac{q^{\varpi_{7}(\eta^{c},N)}\cdot q^{\varpi_{5}(\eta^{r},N,0)}}{q^{\varpi_{5}(\eta,N,1)}\cdot q^{\varpi_{5}(\eta^{rc},N,1)}}=\frac{\prod\limits_{i=1}^{d(\eta)-1}q_{i-N}^{\eta_{i}}}{\prod\limits_{i=1}^{d(\eta)}q_{i-N-1}^{\eta_{i}-1}}.

Proof.

It follows from the similar argument as in the proof of Lemma
4.22
.
∎

4.3.2.
Weights for the graphical condensation recurrence with
\mathbf{G}=\mathbf{H}(N;\lambda^{rc},\mu,\nu^{rc})

The similar argument as in Section 4.3.1 shows that

Lemma 4.24
.

The minimal dimer configuration of
\mathbf{H}(N;\lambda^{rc},\mu,\nu^{rc})-\{a,d\}
has the weight

q^{\omega_{\min}^{LU}(\lambda^{r},\mu,\nu^{c};N)}:=q^{\widetilde{\omega}_{\min}^{LU}(\lambda^{r},\mu,\nu^{c};N)}\cdot\prod\limits_{l=0}^{n-1}q_{l}^{-|\mathrm{II}(\lambda^{r},\mu,\nu^{c})|_{l}}\cdot q_{l}^{-2|\mathrm{III}(\lambda^{r},\mu,\nu^{c})|_{l}}
, where

\displaystyle q^{\widetilde{\omega}^{LU}_{\min}(\lambda^{r},\mu,\nu^{c};N)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\prod_{i=0}^{N}\prod_{j=0}^{N-1}q_{j-i+1}^{N-i}\cdot\prod_{i=1}^{\ell((\lambda^{r})^{\prime})}\prod_{j=0}^{N-1}q_{j-i+2}^{(\lambda^{r})^{\prime}_{i}}\cdot\prod_{i=1}^{\ell(\mu)}\prod_{j=0}^{N}q_{i-j}^{\mu_{i}}\cdot\prod_{i=1}^{\ell(\nu^{c})}\prod_{j=0}^{\nu^{c}_{i}-1}q_{j-i+2}^{N-1}\cdot\prod_{i=1}^{\ell(\nu^{c})}\prod_{j=0}^{\nu^{c}_{i}-1}q_{j-i+2}^{-2N+i}

\displaystyle\times\left(q_{N}^{N+(\lambda^{r})^{\prime}_{1}}\right)^{-1}\cdot\prod_{i:2\leq i\leq(\lambda^{r})^{\prime}_{i}+1}\prod_{j=1}^{i-2}q_{N+j+1-i}^{N+1+(\lambda^{r})^{\prime}_{i}-i}\cdot\prod_{i:(\lambda^{r})^{\prime}_{i}+1<i\leq\ell((\lambda^{r})^{\prime})}\prod_{j=1}^{(\lambda^{r})^{\prime}_{i}}q_{N+j+1-i}^{N+1+(\lambda^{r})^{\prime}_{i}-i}

\displaystyle\times\prod_{i:1\leq i\leq\mu_{i}}\prod_{j=1}^{i-1}q_{i-j-N}^{\mu_{i}-j}\cdot\prod_{i:\mu_{i}<i\leq\ell(\mu)}\prod_{j=1}^{\mu_{i}}q_{i-j-N}^{\mu_{i}-j}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle q^{\varpi_{1}(N,1,1)}\cdot q^{\varpi_{2}((\lambda^{\prime})^{c},N-1,2)}\cdot\overline{q^{\varpi_{2}(\mu,N,0)}}\cdot q^{\varpi_{3}(\nu^{c},N+1,2)}\cdot q^{\varpi_{6}((\lambda^{\prime})^{c},N)}\cdot q^{\varpi_{5}(\mu,N,1)}.

Lemma 4.25
.

The minimal dimer configuration of
\mathbf{H}(N;\lambda^{rc},\mu,\nu^{rc})-\{b,c\}
has the weight

q^{\omega_{\min}^{RD}(\lambda^{c},\mu,\nu^{r};N)}:=q^{\widetilde{\omega}_{\min}^{RD}}(\lambda^{c},\mu,\nu^{r};N)\cdot\prod\limits_{l=0}^{n-1}q_{l}^{|\mathrm{II}(\lambda^{c},\mu,\nu^{r})|_{l}}\cdot q_{l}^{2|\mathrm{III}(\lambda^{c},\mu,\nu^{r})|_{l}}
, where

\displaystyle q^{\widetilde{\omega}^{RD}_{\min}(\lambda^{c},\mu,\nu^{r};n)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\prod_{i=0}^{N-2}\prod_{j=0}^{N-1}q_{j-i-1}^{N-i-2}\cdot\prod_{i=1}^{\ell((\lambda^{c})^{\prime})}\prod_{j=0}^{N-1}q_{j-i}^{(\lambda^{c})^{\prime}_{i}}\cdot\prod_{i=1}^{\ell(\mu)}\prod_{j=0}^{N-2}q_{i-j-2}^{\mu_{i}}\cdot\prod_{i=1}^{\ell(\nu^{r})}\prod_{j=0}^{\nu^{r}_{i}-1}q_{j-i}^{N}\cdot\prod_{i=1}^{\ell(\nu^{r})}\prod_{j=0}^{\nu^{r}_{i}-1}q_{j-i}^{-2N+i+1}

\displaystyle\times\prod_{i:1\leq i\leq(\lambda^{c})_{i}^{\prime}}\prod_{j=1}^{i}q^{N+(\lambda^{c})_{i}^{\prime}-i-1}_{N-i+j-1}\cdot\prod_{i:(\lambda^{c})_{i}^{\prime}<i\leq\ell((\lambda^{c})^{\prime})}\prod_{j=1}^{(\lambda^{c})_{i}^{\prime}}q_{N-i+j-1}^{N+(\lambda^{c})_{i}^{\prime}-i-1}

\displaystyle\times\prod_{i:1\leq i\leq\mu_{i}}\prod_{j=1}^{i-1}q_{i-j-N}^{\mu_{i}-j}\cdot\prod_{i:\mu_{i}<i\leq\ell(\mu)}\prod_{j=1}^{\mu_{i}}q_{i-j-N}^{\mu_{i}-j}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle q^{\varpi_{1}(N-2,-1,-1)}\cdot q^{\varpi_{2}((\lambda^{\prime})^{r},N-1,0)}\cdot\overline{q^{\varpi_{2}(\mu,N-2,2)}}\cdot q^{\varpi_{3}(\nu^{r},N-1,0)}\cdot q^{\varpi_{4}((\lambda^{\prime})^{r},N,0)}\cdot q^{\varpi_{5}(\mu,N,1)}.

In this case, we have

\displaystyle\frac{q^{\widetilde{\omega}_{\min}(\lambda^{rc},\mu,\nu;N)}\cdot q^{\widetilde{\omega}_{\min}(\lambda,\mu,\nu^{rc};N)}}{q^{\widetilde{\omega}_{\min}(\lambda,\mu,\nu;N)}\cdot q^{\widetilde{\omega}_{\min}(\lambda^{rc},\mu,\nu^{rc};N)}}=1

and still require the following lemmas to derive the graphical condensation recurrence (
8
).

Lemma 4.26
.

For any partitions
\lambda,\nu\neq\emptyset
and
\mu
, we have

\displaystyle\frac{q^{\widetilde{\omega}_{\min}^{LU}(\lambda^{r},\mu,\nu^{c};N)}\cdot q^{\widetilde{\omega}_{\min}^{RD}(\lambda^{c},\mu,\nu^{r};N)}}{q^{\widetilde{\omega}_{\min}(\lambda,\mu,\nu;N)}\cdot q^{\widetilde{\omega}_{\min}(\lambda^{rc},\mu,\nu^{rc};N)}}=q^{K_{2}(\lambda,\mu,\nu)}

where

\displaystyle q^{K_{2}(\lambda,\mu,\nu)}:=q_{1-d(\lambda^{\prime})}^{-\lambda^{\prime}_{d(\lambda^{\prime})}}\cdot\prod\limits_{i=1}^{d(\lambda^{\prime})-1}q_{-i}\cdot\prod\limits_{i=1}^{d(\lambda^{\prime})-1}\left(\frac{q_{-i}}{q_{-i+1}}\right)^{\lambda^{\prime}_{i}}\cdot\prod\limits_{i=1}^{\ell(\mu)}\left(\frac{q_{i}}{q_{i-1}}\right)^{\mu_{i}}\cdot\prod\limits_{i=1}^{\nu_{d(\nu)}-d(\nu)}q_{i}^{-1}

is independent of
N
.

Proof.

It follows from Lemmas
4.14
,
4.24
,
4.25
,
4.27
,
4.28
,
4.29
,
4.30
,
4.22
.
∎

The following lemmas follow from the similar argument in Section 4.3.1.

Lemma 4.27
.

\displaystyle\frac{q^{\varpi_{1}(N,1,1)}\cdot q^{\varpi_{1}(N-2,-1,-1)}}{\left(q^{\varpi_{1}(N-1,0,0)}\right)^{2}}=q_{0}^{1-N}\cdot q_{N}^{N}\cdot\prod\limits_{i=1}^{N-1}q_{i}.

Lemma 4.28
.

For any partition
\eta\neq\emptyset
, we have

\displaystyle\frac{q^{\varpi_{2}(\eta^{c},N-1,2)}q^{\varpi_{2}(\eta^{r},N-1,0)}}{q^{\varpi_{2}(\eta,N-1,1)}q^{\varpi_{2}(\eta^{rc},N-1,1)}}=q_{1-d(\eta)}^{-\eta_{d(\eta)}}\cdot\prod\limits_{i=1}^{d(\eta)-1}\left(\frac{q_{-i}}{q_{-i+1}}\right)^{\eta_{i}}\cdot\frac{\prod\limits_{i=1}^{d(\eta)}q_{N-i+1}^{\eta_{i}-1}\cdot\prod\limits_{i=1}^{d(\eta)-1}q_{-i}}{\prod\limits_{i=1}^{d(\eta)-1}q_{N-i}^{\eta_{i}}\cdot\prod\limits_{i=1}^{N-1}q_{i}}.

Lemma 4.29
.

For any partition
\eta
, we have

\displaystyle\frac{q^{\varpi_{2}(\eta,N,0)}q^{\varpi_{2}(\eta,N-2,2)}}{\left(q^{\varpi_{2}(\eta,N-1,1)}\right)^{2}}=\prod\limits_{i=1}^{\ell(\eta)}\left(\frac{q_{-i}}{q_{-i+1}}\right)^{\eta_{i}}.

Lemma 4.30
.

For any partition
\eta\neq\emptyset
, we have

\displaystyle\frac{q^{\varpi_{3}(\eta^{c},N+1,2)}q^{\varpi_{3}(\eta^{r},N-1,0)}}{q^{\varpi_{3}(\eta,N,1)}q^{\varpi_{3}(\eta^{rc},N,1)}}=q_{0}^{N-1}\cdot\prod\limits_{i=1}^{\eta_{d(\eta)}-d(\eta)}q_{i}^{-1}.

4.3.3.
Weights for the graphical condensation recurrence with
\mathbf{G}=\mathbf{H}(N;\lambda,\mu^{rc},\nu^{rc})

Again, by the similar argument as in Section 4.3.1 we have

Lemma 4.31
.

The minimal dimer configuration of
\mathbf{H}(N;\lambda,\mu^{rc},\nu^{rc})-\{a,d\}
has the weight

q^{\omega_{\min}^{LD}(\lambda,\mu^{r},\nu^{c};N)}:=q^{\widetilde{\omega}_{\min}^{LD}(\lambda,\mu^{r},\nu^{c};N)}\cdot\prod\limits_{l=0}^{n-1}q_{l}^{-|\mathrm{II}(\lambda,\mu^{r},\nu^{c})|_{l}}\cdot q_{l}^{-2|\mathrm{III}(\lambda,\mu^{r},\nu^{c})|_{l}}
, where

\displaystyle q^{\widetilde{\omega}^{LD}_{\min}(\lambda,\mu^{r},\nu^{c};N)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\prod_{i=0}^{N-1}\prod_{j=0}^{N-2}q_{j-i+1}^{N-i-1}\cdot\prod_{i=1}^{\ell(\lambda^{\prime})}\prod_{j=0}^{N-2}q_{j-i+2}^{\lambda^{\prime}_{i}}\cdot\prod_{i=1}^{\ell(\mu^{r})}\prod_{j=0}^{N-1}q_{i-j}^{\mu^{r}_{i}}\cdot\prod_{i=1}^{\ell(\nu^{c})}\prod_{j=0}^{\nu^{c}_{i}-1}q_{j-i+2}^{N}\cdot\prod_{i=1}^{\ell(\nu^{c})}\prod_{j=0}^{\nu^{c}_{i}-1}q_{j-i+2}^{-2N+i}

\displaystyle\times\prod_{i:1\leq i\leq\lambda^{\prime}_{i}}\prod_{j=1}^{i-1}q_{N+j-i}^{N+\lambda^{\prime}_{i}-i}\cdot\prod_{i:\lambda^{\prime}_{i}<i\leq\ell(\lambda^{\prime})}\prod_{j=1}^{\lambda^{\prime}_{i}}q_{N+j-i}^{N+\lambda^{\prime}_{i}-i}

\displaystyle\times\prod_{i:1\leq i\leq\mu^{r}_{i}}\prod_{j=1}^{i}q_{i-j-N+1}^{\mu^{r}_{i}-j}\cdot\prod_{i:\mu^{r}_{i}<i\leq\ell(\mu^{r})}\prod_{j=1}^{\mu^{r}_{i}}q_{i-j-N+1}^{\mu^{r}_{i}-j}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle q^{\varpi_{1}(N-1,1,1)}\cdot q^{\varpi_{2}(\lambda^{\prime},N-2,2)}\cdot\overline{q^{\varpi_{2}(\mu^{r},N-1,0)}}\cdot q^{\varpi_{3}(\nu^{c},N,2)}\cdot q^{\varpi_{4}(\lambda^{\prime},N,1)}\cdot q^{\varpi_{5}(\mu^{r},N,0)}.

Lemma 4.32
.

The minimal dimer configuration of
\mathbf{H}(N;\lambda,\mu^{rc},\nu^{rc})-\{b,c\}
has the weight

q^{\omega_{\min}^{RU}(\lambda,\mu^{c},\nu^{r};N)}:=q^{\widetilde{\omega}_{\min}^{RU}}(\lambda,\mu^{c},\nu^{r};N)\cdot\prod\limits_{l=0}^{n-1}q_{l}^{-|\mathrm{II}(\lambda,\mu^{c},\nu^{r})|_{l}}\cdot q_{l}^{-2|\mathrm{III}(\lambda,\mu^{c},\nu^{r})|_{l}}
, where

\displaystyle q^{\widetilde{\omega}^{RU}_{\min}(\lambda,\mu^{c},\nu^{r};N)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\prod_{i=0}^{N-1}\prod_{j=0}^{N}q_{j-i-1}^{N-i-1}\cdot\prod_{i=1}^{\ell(\lambda^{\prime})}\prod_{j=0}^{N}q_{j-i}^{\lambda^{\prime}_{i}}\cdot\prod_{i=1}^{\ell(\mu^{c})}\prod_{j=0}^{N-1}q_{i-j-2}^{\mu^{c}_{i}}\cdot\prod_{i=1}^{\ell(\nu^{r})}\prod_{j=0}^{\nu^{r}_{i}-1}q_{j-i}^{N-1}\cdot\prod_{i=1}^{\ell(\nu^{r})}\prod_{j=0}^{\nu^{r}_{i}-1}q_{j-i}^{-2N+i+1}
